Answers render through `components/AiAnswer` (Markdown + typewriter, then every act / code / CAEN / court file / CUI marked `.ai-lref` in its platform's colour; `onRef(hit)` — Research opens its drawer, the viewer the main window's Legislation tab). The viewer's automatic source-correction turn, its choices rule, sources rule, persona and rule folding were REMOVED (the source-check card stays). **SPEED (2026-09-28, second pass):** questions are STREAMED (project-ai `stream: true` → its own SSE `{ t: 'text' | 'done' | 'error' }`; lib/projectAi `askProjectAiStream`, which reads a plain JSON answer too, so it works against a function not yet redeployed; Research keeps the live text in lib/researchRunner `stream`, the viewer in `streamText`; `AiAnswer streaming`; Stop aborts the request); PROMPT CACHING — the stable data travels apart as `context` (`prepareTurn` → `{ context, data }`: the open file + project digest vs the portal records) and the server lays the request out stable → volatile with breakpoints after the system prompt, after the `context` system block and on the last assistant turn; `warmTurn` writes that prefix with max_tokens 0 while the question is typed (≤ once per model + data per 4 min, usageAction `ai-warm`); `effort: 'medium'` on every turn (constant, so the cache holds); Auto's `quickRoute` settles obvious questions locally, the router waits ≤4s; portals ≤6s; the project's files are listed only when the digest is rebuilt; the viewer reads named files in parallel. The usage carries `cache_read_input_tokens` / `cache_creation_input_tokens` (in each Research answer's `timing`). **MANNERS** (`AI_MANNERS`, the user's etiquette instructions word for word): every conversational answer — Research, its AI summaries, the file viewer — carries them as the first part of the cached `context` — plus the FORMAL ROMANIAN ADDRESS rule (`<romanian_address>`: "dumneavoastră" and 2nd-person plural in every pronoun, verb, imperative, possessive, reflexive, greeting and closing — "ce doriți", never "ce dorești") (`withManners`, the warm-up too); `askAi({ manners: false })` leaves them out where code reads the answer (paragraph rewrites); a scope line keeps greetings out of documents and files. **ANSWER STYLES** (`AI_STYLES`: Claude = nothing added, DocVex = formal law-firm register with no emojis, Direct = just the information, still courteous (a brief greeting and closing kept, only padding dropped); `docvex:ai:style:v1`, one setting) — `AiStylePicker` in the composer of both, right of the model picker (Auto); the style line rides at the END of a question only (never drafts / paragraph edits). **THE PLAYBOOK DECIDES how a document is created AND edited:** the file viewer's stable context carries `docRulesSteer()` as `<playbook_rules>` on every turn (drafts, paragraph edits, `docvex-edit` file edits — cached with the rest), the writing voice rides on drafts only (`withVoice`), and project-ai's former `PLACEHOLDER_RULE` (named `[[…]]` blanks, never deployed) was REPLACED by `PLAYBOOK_RULE`, which defers to the user's rules (the tool description, the document-builder system prompt and the `generate` action). Debug → **AI settings** (`pages/DebugAiSettings`) shows all of it (incl. Speed & caching and Answer styles), every prompt word for word, and the other AIs' prompts. `lib/researchAi` / `lib/researchModels` were folded in and deleted. NOT verified in the app. |

| `legalDetectionArchive.js` | **Everything the user gave about detecting Romanian legislation, VERBATIM, frozen 2026-09-28** so it is never lost: their source texts (Legea 24/2000 citation rules, the I–XI identifier catalogue, the ONRC CAEN list, the CUI line), their instructions, the rules as documented here, every core regex (`LAW_REF_REGEXES` in lib/lawRefs) and catalogue entry, and a copy of lib/lawRefs + lib/legalOmni. Generated once; add to it, never rewrite it. Shown in Debug → **Legislation detection — the archive** (`pages/DebugLegalArchive`), which flags a pattern that has changed or left the code since, and downloads it as JSON. |
| `aiInventory.js` | **Every AI the app uses, as DATA** — providers (`AI_PROVIDERS`: Anthropic, OpenAI, with keys and training posture), the Edge Functions and their uses (`AI_FUNCTIONS`, each use `{ id (usageAction), surface, task, model, what }`), the surfaces (`AI_SURFACES`), the tasks (`AI_TASKS`), what runs locally (`AI_LOCAL`). Shown in the Debug tab (`AiInventory`) by service, by surface or by TASK — a task done on two or more surfaces is marked "merge candidate", the groundwork for unifying the AIs (Doc Viewer quick tools, Advisor, Research…). **A new AI use = an entry here.** |
| `liveNetwork.js` + `components/LiveNetworkRunner` | **THE LIVE NEURAL NETWORK (2026-09-28)** — a file added to the project is understood within seconds and linked into the network, with no scan pressed. Trigger: the index's `project:delta` (Import, phone, Explorer, sync alike; a reconcile of >100 rows is not an arrival; dotfiles, `~$` temp files, `.dvc` / `.docvex` / `.tmp` / `.part` ignored); arrivals SETTLE (1.8s quiet, ≤8s) and a burst is one pass; one pass at a time per project; a manual scan in progress is waited for. An arrival is TAGGED for the AI scan (lib/scanTags — AI mark, removable as ever; a MOVE keeps the file's tag by its portable id) and kept in a per-device pending list (`docvex:live-network:pending:v1:<dir>`), and the pass is `scanProjectFiles(…, { only: pending })`: files the web knows are still checked (edits / removals of scanned files trigger a pass too), unknown tagged files outside `only` are left for a manual scan. The pass shows in the scan gauges and the sidebar spinner only once it has real work (`live: true` on the state, 'Live ·' in the card); a quiet toast 'Understood N new files'. **CONSENT** per project, per device, OFF by default (`docvex:live-network:v1:<dir>` → `{ on, recordings, paused }`): the scan card's **Live neural network** section (`LiveSection` in components/ScanGauges, `scanDir` prop from ProjectFiles → FilesWorkspace → ScanButton): Understand new files automatically (says it sends them to the AI; files already there are not swept up), Include audio & video (a second consent), and the **Running / Paused** switch — pausing stops a pass at the next file (what was read is kept) and resuming runs what waited; the card shows the waiting count. **TAGGING queues too** (with or without the consent; Pause holds it): tagging a file or a FOLDER queues everything it covers at once (`subscribeScanTags` → newly covered files into `pending` + `asked`, `docvex:live-network:asked:v1:<dir>`), a file added later inside a tagged folder is queued on arrival, and untagging runs a pass that drops those files from the collections. The runner is mounted in AppShell (main window only), locating the selected project's folder through the index. **Speed** (dataCollections): the scan's FAST PATH returns before hydrating or reading any collection when every listed file is known with the same stamp and none has gone; collections are read through `COLLECTION_CACHE` (size + mtime, cloned out); and cross-referencing a group that is an earlier group plus ≤8 new files sends only the new files + their 12 likeliest matches (`deltaPlan` — group answers now carry `files` with stamps; the earlier answers stand for the rest). Verified in Node: a file joining a 30-file group → 13 passports sent, 1 call; again → 0 calls. NOT verified in the app. |
| `docxRenderCache.js` | **The Doc Viewer's Word render cache (2026-09-28)** — a .docx opens from the pages it was laid out as last time: the finished stage's HTML (base64 images / fonts, every mark a `data-` attribute) + page width, keyed by the file's SHA-1 + document theme + folded headings (`docxContentKey`), so an edit, a theme or a fold is another entry. The render effect in `DocxRenderPane` checks it first (`getDocxRender`) and skips docx-preview, the font wait and the pagination; a fresh render is stored after pagination (`putDocxRender`, idle-time). Memory (8 per window) + IndexedDB `docvex-render-cache` (`docx` pages, `meta` sizes / times; ≤60 entries / 250 MB, ≤30 MB each, oldest out) shared by every viewer window and kept across restarts; Debug → Clear all cached data clears it. Bump `VERSION` when the pagination or the marks change shape. NOT verified in the app. |
| `cloudMedia.js` | **Cloud reading of images and audio — per project, per device, OFF by default (2026-09-28).** `isCloudMediaAllowed(projectId = the window's selected project)` (set by SelectedProjectContext, `setCloudMediaProject`), `setCloudMediaAllowed`, `subscribeCloudMedia`; the switch is the AI scan card's **Privacy** section (`CloudMediaSection` in components/ScanGauges). Off: `lib/ocr` `recognizeCanvas` reads on this computer (PaddleOCR `readLines`) — the viewer's lasso, the snip overlay and `readSourceText` / `readIdentityFromImage` (option `cloudOcr`, default the switch; a local reading is saved as the `ocr` facet with engine `paddleocr`); Extract text's AI strips are skipped (local reading); `transcribeAudio(…, { projectId })` throws `cloudMediaOffError()` (no local transcription — the scan skips the rest of the recordings); `pictureToWord` answers `{ ok: false, code: 'cloud_media_off' }`; `readMrzWithAi` answers null; AI search leaves pictures undescribed. It governs IMAGES AND AUDIO only — extracted TEXT still reaches the AI through the features that send it (see lib/pseudonymize). NOT verified in the app. |
| `pseudonymize/` (`vault.js`, `detectorLayer1.js`, `detectorLayer2.js`, `wire.js`, `transport.js`, `storage.js`) + `pseudonymizeSetting.js` | **The pseudonymisation vault (2026-09-28) — WIRED at the transport layer.** lib/projectAi masks every text call's body and re-identifies its answer when `isPseudonymizeOn(projectId, usageAction)`: `askProjectAi`, `askProjectAiStream` (the stream re-identified as it arrives by `makeStreamReidentifier` — a trailing unclosed `[` or partial `TYPE_NN` is held back, so no token is ever shown split), `warmProjectAi` (masked exactly as the turn, or the cache never matches), `passportFiles`, `crossrefPassports`; callers keep seeing real values. `maskBody` (wire.js) masks content strings only — structural keys (`action`, `model`, `id`, `role`, `type`, `tool_use_id`, `media_type`…) and BINARY blocks (`type: image | document`, any `source`) are passed through untouched. The vault and its detectors are LAZY-loaded (projectAi decides with the cheap setting first) — they pull lib/lawRefs, which must stay out of the startup bundle. **Setting** (`pseudonymizeSetting.js`, per project, per device, `docvex:pseudonymize:v1:<id>`): `default` = the AI scan (`files-scan`) and the MRZ reader (`mrz`) only, `all`, `off` — the AI scan card's Privacy section (a Segmented choice). **Failure**: no project / vault storage refused (safeStorage unavailable — main's `vault:get` now answers `no_encryption` BEFORE reading — or a preload without the bridge) FAILS CLOSED for `files-scan` / `mrz` (`vault_unavailable`, nothing sent) and goes out unmasked with a console warning for the rest (`transport.js` `vaultForCall`). The MRZ reader makes no call while masked (the strip would arrive as `[MRZ_01]`). **Layer 2** (`detectorLayer2.js` `makeLayer2(entities)`, `knownEntitiesFrom({ records, parties })`): known people by their WHOLE name in any order, case, with or without diacritics (folded character by character, same length, so spans map back), the last word also declined (-ului, -lui, -ei, -ii, -ul, -a, -i → base / +u / +a / +e); a surname alone never matches; companies by their core words in order, the legal form before / after (S.C., S.R.L., SA… dotted or not) taken into the span, a one-word company only beside its form; each span `link`s to the CNP / CUI. `refreshKnownEntities` (storage.js; the project's records via `listProjectIdentities` + the scan's parties from the web index; at most once a minute, a call waits ≤4 s) links every name + CNP / CUI pair in the vault up front. A registered value's metadata is filled in by its first sighting. **MERGE (5a)**: two entities of one family found to be one (`link`, or a span whose `link` points at another entity) are merged — `mergeEntities` keeps the LOWER number, `merge(keepKey, dropKey)` moves values / aliases / metadata and re-points the index; `redirects` (dropped key → kept, stored — format `v: 2`, a `v: 1` vault reads with none) make a token sent under the old number re-identify for good (`resolveKey`). A company's normalised form ignores its legal form (SC / SRL / S.R.L. / SA…), so a record and a guess of the same firm are one entity. **LAYER 3 (5b)** (`detectorLayer3.js` `detectLayer3`): names the project does NOT know, from Romanian cues only — a person = 2–4 capitalised / ALL-CAPS words (hyphens, diacritics) after subsemnatul/-a, domnul/doamna, dl./dna., numitul/-a, reprezentat(ă) prin, născut(ă), fiul/fiica lui, moștenitorul/-oarea, a party label (Vânzător:, Cumpărător:, Pârât:, Reclamant:, Client:…) or BEFORE „, în calitate de”; a company = 1–5 capitalised words with S.C. before or a legal form after (leading label words trimmed); words never part of a name (`NEVER`: months, days, courts / laws / institutions, street types, counties and big towns, party labels, legal forms) end it. No link. Rides per call (`mask(text, { detectors })`, `maskBody(body, vault, opts)`), after Layer 2 (an equal span keeps the known name); `isGuessNamesOn(projectId, usageAction)` — the calls masked by default (`files-scan`, `mrz`) unless switched off (`setGuessNames`, the card's 'Also guess names the project doesn't know'). Citations, dates and amounts are now off limits to EVERY layer (the vault filters the other layers' spans with `protectedSpans`). **WHAT WAS SENT (5c)** (`sentLog.js`): the last 30 AI calls of this window, in memory — `{ at, usageAction, projectId, masked, sent, reason?, bodyPreview }`, `bodyPreview` = the MASKED text that went out (`bodyText` in wire.js: content strings only, binary blocks named, ≤4000 chars) and null for a call sent unmasked or refused (the log never holds real data); recorded by projectAi's `masked()` for every call (warm-ups excluded) and by transport's fail-closed refusals; shown under the card's Privacy section (`SentLog`: Masked / Not masked / Not sent pills, the text on a click, Clear). `Vault` (per project, deterministic): `getOrCreateToken(type, value, meta, { link })`, `link(a, b)`, `resolveToken`, `reidentify` (deep: strings, arrays, plain objects, keys; tolerates `[CNP_07 · F · 1985]`, `[CNP_07]`, `CNP_07`, other separators; unknown tokens left), `mask(text)`, `maskDeep(body)`, `save()` (serialised writes), `clear()`, `Vault.open(projectId, { storage, detectors })`. ONE NUMBER PER ENTITY, the TYPE says which value: `PERSOANA_07` (name) / `CNP_07 · F · 1985` / `CI_07`, `FIRMA_03` / `CUI_03`, and `IBAN`, `MRZ`, `TEL`, `EMAIL`, `ADRESA_05 · <county> · <locality>`; values normalised before lookup (`normalizeValue`: CNP / phone digits, IBAN / MRZ upper-case without spaces, e-mail lower-case, names folded with words sorted). Extra detectors plug in via `detectors` (Layer 2). `detectLayer1(text)` → non-overlapping spans, longest wins: CNP (after the word whatever it reads as; bare only with a valid check digit), CUI (lib/lawRefs `findCuiRefs`, the number only), IBAN (mod-97, `ibanValid`), CI series + number and passport numbers, MRZ lines (`isMrzLine`), Romanian phones (10 national digits, +40 / 0040 folded), e-mails, addresses after domiciliat / cu sediul / adresa… (cut at the next clause; meta from `parseAddress`); nothing overlapping a citation of law (`findLawRefs`), a date or an amount (`protectedSpans`; an address is dropped only over a citation). STORAGE: main's `vault:get` / `vault:put` (preload `vaultGet` / `vaultPut`) — `<userData>/vault/<projectId>.bin`, `safeStorage`-encrypted, written atomically, never written when encryption is unavailable, never in a case folder or .docvex/; `openProjectVault(projectId)` (storage.js) — one per window, last write wins across windows (a lost write only renumbers, never restores a wrong value). Main changed → restart `npm start`. Tests: `tests/pseudonymize.test.mjs` (24, `npm test` — plus merge: a late link, old and new tokens, reopen, v1 read; Layer 3: cues found, courts / laws / streets / months / places left, a guess merged with a record, a known name winning; the sent log: masked text only, 30 max — plus Layer 2: names masked in the three documents, a name sharing its CNP's / CUI's number, word orders / cases / diacritics / a declined last word, no false positive on a surname alone, a street or a common word; the transport: maskBody leaving structure and a picture's bytes alone, and the stream re-identified identically for EVERY cut point and character by character with no token fragment shown): three mock documents (ID card with MRZ, sale contract, invoice), nothing identifying left, citations / dates / amounts kept, determinism across documents and a reopened vault, linked name + CNP, deep re-identification of a mangled AI graph, mask∘reidentify stable. (`tests/projectIndexClient.test.mjs` has 2 failures that predate this — they fail on the committed HEAD too.) |
| `oneOpen.js` | **One dropdown open at a time**, app-wide: `useOneOpen(open, close)` — a component announces its opening (a `docvex:one-open` window event carrying its own id) and every other open one closes. With `refs` (the field and what it opens) a press OUTSIDE them closes it too; BarPicker and BarCalendar have their own outside-press close (BarCalendar's now covers the gallery's inline pickers as well). Used by LegalBar's `BarPicker`, every BarCalendar date field (shut at once, no fade, so two never overlap) and the Design system's Dropdown sample. The gallery's dropdowns and calendars all start CLOSED. A new dropdown should call it. |

## Supabase data model

Project ID `pntxlvhkqfryyyxlqytr` (eu-west-1, organization `docvex.ro`). Modify via the `claude_ai_Supabase` MCP tools (`list_tables`, `apply_migration`, etc.). **No cloud file store** — the `drop_branching_and_cloud_files` migration (2026-06-02, "migration 031") dropped the tables `project_files`, `change_requests`, `change_request_items`, `branch_changes`, `project_member_branches`, the `projects.main_version` column, and the branching RPCs. The `projects` / `projects-pending` **storage buckets still physically exist** in the project but are orphaned — the app no longer reads or writes them (files are local-only). They're a cleanup candidate, not a live dependency.

Migrations continued **after** the branching drop (it is NOT the latest): the admin stack (`admin_stats_rpc`, `app_admins_table_and_rpcs`, `app_services_inventory`), the Mail stack (`create_user_mail_connections`, `create_mail_oauth_states`), and `create_enrollments` (the current latest, 2026-06-17). Use `list_migrations` for the authoritative order.

### Tables (current)

| Table | Notable columns | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `projects` | `id, name, description, created_by, created_at, updated_at, ai_context?, ai_context_updated_at?` | `add_creator_as_owner` trigger inserts owner row. `ai_context*` (migration 030) feeds the Project AI tab — admin-writable. |
| `project_members` | PK `(project_id, user_id)`, `role` enum (`owner/admin/member/viewer`), `custom_role_id?`, `added_at` | RLS via `has_project_role` / `has_capability`. |
| `project_invitations` | `id, project_id, email, role, custom_role_id?, token unique, invited_by, created_at, expires_at (+7d), accepted_at?` | Unique on `(project_id, lower(email))` WHERE `accepted_at IS NULL`. Consumed by `accept-invite` Edge Function → `accept_invitation` RPC. |
| `custom_roles` | `id, project_id, name, description, base_role` (`admin/member/viewer`, owner excluded) | Migration 008. |
| `custom_role_capabilities` | PK `(custom_role_id, capability)`, `granted bool` | `project_capability` enum: `files.view/upload/delete_any/delete_own`, `members.invite/remove/change_role`. Row absence = inherit from base tier. |
| `project_ai_usage` | `id, project_id, user_id?, action, model, input_tokens, output_tokens, session_id?, created_at` | Migration 030. Backs the Project AI usage panel via `get_project_ai_usage`. |
| `notifications` | `id, user_id, category, variant, priority, title, body, icon?, payload jsonb, created_at, read_at?, dedupe_key?` | Unique `(user_id, dedupe_key)`. Index `(user_id, created_at DESC)`. |
| `chat_messages` | `id, project_id, author_id, body, mentions uuid[], attached_file_ids uuid[], created_at, edited_at?, deleted_at?, parent_id?, pinned_at?, pinned_by?` | Soft-delete via `deleted_at`. `parent_id` / pin columns + `chat_message_reactions` table from migration 026. |
| `private_messages` | — | DM messages; see `lib/privateMessages.js`. |
| `legal_updates` | `id, slug unique, category, impact (low/medium/high), title, source?, citations?, summary?, areas text[], raw_content?, ai_status, published_at, created_at, updated_at` + (037) `origin ('portal'/'manual'), portal_id?, act_type?, act_number?, act_year?, source_url?` | Global Legal Newsfeed, not project-scoped. Migration 029. Public read (`for select using (true)`); written by `legal-feed-sync` (from Monitorul Oficial), `legal-ai` ingest, or seed. The 11 rows seeded by 029 are hand-written SAMPLES (`origin='manual'`), not real acts. |
| `legal_feed_acts` | `portal_id` PK, `act_type, act_number, title, issuer, mo_ref, mo_date, in_force, link, status (skipped/rejected/relevant/ingested/failed), category, reason, attempts, error` | Migration 037. Every act `legal-feed-sync` has judged — the audit trail for "why isn't X in the feed". Service role only (RLS on, no policies). `legal_feed_state` (key/value) sits beside it. |
| `legal_update_states` | PK `(user_id, update_id)`, `read_at?, pinned_at?, saved_at?, updated_at` | Per-user read/pin/save flags. Migration 029. RLS: `user_id = auth.uid()`. |
| `chat_message_reactions` | `(message_id, user_id, emoji)` | Emoji reactions on `chat_messages` (migration 026). Toggled via `lib/chat.js`. |
| `app_admins` | app-admin allowlist | Backs `is_app_admin` / the Admin page gate. Not project-scoped. |
| `app_services` | service-inventory rows | Powers the Admin "Developer Console" service tracker (Supabase/Anthropic/Resend/domains). |
| `user_mail_connections` | per-user mailbox tokens (encrypted at rest) | Gmail/Outlook OAuth connections for the Mail tab; tokens AES-GCM-encrypted by `mail-sync` (`MAIL_TOKEN_KEY`). |
| `mail_oauth_states` | OAuth nonce/state rows | Short-lived CSRF/state records for the Mail OAuth round-trip (`mail-callback`). |
| `phone_upload_sessions` / `phone_upload_files` | session: `user_id, project_id?, token_hash (sha256), expires_at, closed_at?, file_count, byte_count`; file: `session_id, path, name, size, mime` | Migration 040 (+ 041: `status` waiting/accepted/rejected, `taken_at`, `decided_at` on files; owner UPDATE policy — rows outlive the hand-off to carry the decision back to the phone; sessions KEPT 7 days sliding and REOPENED by `create` with the kept token). Upload from a phone through the cloud (the Files tab's Import). Owner reads/deletes; rows written only by the `phone-upload` Edge Function. `phone_upload_files` is in the Realtime publication. Bucket `phone-upload` (private, 50 MB). |
| `enrollments` | `id, type, name, email, firm, message, created_at` | Website waitlist / demo / contact forms (anon insert). Deleted after 24 months (043). Newsletter sign-ups are NOT here any more. |
| `newsletter_subscribers` | `email unique, status pending/confirmed, confirm_hash, unsubscribe_token, consent_text, consent_source, requested_at, confirmed_at, last_sent_at` | Migration 044. Double opt-in via the `newsletter` function; service role only. |
| `project_folder_keys` | `project_id` PK (cascade), `key` (base64, 32 bytes), `created_at` | Migration 045. Never read directly — `get_project_folder_key(project_id)` (members only, creates on first use). Seals `.docvex/` and sync bundles. |

### RPCs

| RPC | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `has_project_role(project_id, min_role)` | STABLE tier-check used by RLS. |
| `has_capability(project_id, capability)` | SECURITY DEFINER, custom-role-aware capability check (migration 008); supersedes `has_project_role` for files/members RLS. |
| `accept_invitation(token, user_id)` | SECURITY DEFINER. Atomically inserts `project_members` (propagating `custom_role_id`) + marks `accepted_at`. |
| `create_custom_role` / `update_custom_role` | SECURITY INVOKER. Atomically write a custom role + replace its capability overrides. |
| `get_member_profiles` / `get_member_profiles_status` | `(p_project_id uuid)` — joins `project_members` with auth user metadata (+ status). |
| `get_project_ai_usage(project_id, since?)` | SECURITY INVOKER, defaults `since` to start of current month. Monthly usage aggregate for the Project AI tab. |
| `get_admin_stats` | SECURITY DEFINER, email-allowlisted. Admin-page live metrics — see `lib/admin.js`. |
| `is_app_admin` | SECURITY DEFINER. True when the caller is in the `app_admins` allowlist — gates the Admin page + admin RPCs. |
| `current_user_has_password` | Whether the signed-in user has a password set (vs. OAuth-only) — drives the Account "set/change password" affordance. |
| `set_chat_message_pin` | Pin/unpin a chat message (migration 026). |

**Dropped in migration 031 — do not use:** `approve_change_request`, `approve_change_requests`, `_apply_change_request_items`, `reject_change_request`, `reject_change_request_item`.

### RLS patterns

- Project-scoped reads/writes call `has_project_role(...)` or `has_capability(...)`; deletes typically require admin/owner.
- Personal rows (`notifications`, `legal_update_states`) gate on `user_id = auth.uid()`.
- `accept_invitation` and `delete-user` (Edge Function) bypass RLS via SECURITY DEFINER.

### Storage

Project files are local-only (`lib/localFolder.js`, `.docvex.json` sidecar via `lib/localBranchMeta.js`) — the app reads/writes no Supabase Storage bucket. The legacy `projects` / `projects-pending` buckets still exist in the project but are orphaned (see the data-model note above). Live buckets: `email-assets` (public, the email Edge Functions) and **`project-sync`** (private, migration 035) — the per-project copy an account keeps for its other devices, written only for projects whose owner switched "Sync with account" on (`lib/projectSync.js`). Its RLS reads for any member and writes on the `files.upload` / `files.delete_any` capability, keyed on the object's first path segment (`public.storage_project_id`), so sync can't get around a viewer's read-only role. Migration 036 adds a `user-<auth uid>/` folder in the same bucket readable and writable by its owner only (`public.storage_sync_owner_folder`) — the private data bundle.

### AI providers + training posture

Docvex handles privileged legal material, so **no provider in this stack may
train on what users send.** That is a property of the *contract and endpoint*,
not of the model id — there is no "trains" vs "doesn't train" variant of a
model to switch between, and changing `claude-opus-4-7` to something else does
nothing for it. What matters is which company receives the bytes and under
which terms. The full list of third parties that ever see user content:

| Endpoint | Reached from | Sees | Training posture |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `api.anthropic.com/v1/messages` | `project-ai`, `doc-ai`, `legal-ai`, `legal-assist` | document text, chat, OCR images, AI-search file stills | Commercial API terms: inputs/outputs **not** used for training. ~30-day retention for trust & safety; ZDR negotiable. |
| `api.anthropic.com/v1/files` + code-execution container | `project-ai` `office` action | generated Office files | Same commercial terms. |
| `api.openai.com/v1/audio/transcriptions` | `doc-ai` `transcribe` | recorded audio / video audio tracks | API traffic **not** used for training by default (since 2023-03); 30-day retention. |

Rules for anything added later:

- **API endpoints only, never a consumer surface.** Consumer tiers (claude.ai,
  chatgpt.com) *do* train on conversations. Everything here is `api.*` with a
  server-held key, and it must stay that way.
- **Deepgram was removed (2026-09-28).** `doc-ai` used it for speaker
  diarization; its standard (non-Enterprise) terms allow training on the audio
  it receives, so it was taken out entirely rather than disclosed. Captions
  carry no speaker labels server-side (the client's silence-gap heuristic
  separates speakers). `DEEPGRAM_API_KEY` / `DOC_AI_ALLOW_DEEPGRAM` no longer do
  anything and can be deleted from the secrets.
- **New provider ⇒ check its default, not its marketing.** If the default is
  "we may train unless you opt out", don't use it (Deepgram was removed for
  exactly this), and add a row to the table above and to the DPA's sub-processor list.
- Zero-Data-Retention is the remaining upgrade for Anthropic + OpenAI: both
  keep API payloads ~30 days for abuse monitoring unless a ZDR agreement is in
  place. Not the same thing as training, but it's the next thing a firm's
  security review will ask about.

### Edge Functions (`supabase/functions/`)

`accept-invite`, `send-invite`, `revoke-invite`, `send-welcome`, `send-support-report`, `delete-user` — shared HTML email templates in `_shared/emailTemplates.ts`; SMTP via Supabase, fire-and-forget from the client. `delete-user` also disconnects a connected mailbox first (asks `mail-sync` `disconnect` with the caller's JWT, which revokes the Google grant). `mail-sync` refuses to connect a mailbox while `MAIL_TOKEN_KEY` is unset (tokens are never stored in clear) and re-encrypts any legacy plaintext row it loads. `project-ai`'s `office` action DELETES the generated file from Anthropic's Files API right after downloading it (the Files API keeps files until deleted). "Report a problem" attaches its screenshot only when the user ticks the box (ReportProblemModal `attachShot`, off by default). The ANAF map (components/MapDrawer) loads Google Maps only after the user agrees once per device (`docvex:maps-consent:v1`). The website self-hosts its fonts (`landing/home/fonts/`, from @fontsource) and supabase-js (`landing/home/vendor/supabase-js.js`, esbuild bundle) — no Google Fonts / esm.sh request; re-bundle the vendor file when upgrading.

`legal-feed-sync` — **the Newsletter's real source** (migrations 037 + 038). legislatie.just.ro geo-blocks Supabase's servers (drops every connection from eu-west-1; its nginx stamps `X-Country`/`X-Block`), so the function NEVER contacts the portal: the **desktop app** reads it (`lib/legalFeedSync.js`, mounted as `LegalFeedSyncRunner` in App.jsx — main window, app admins only, checked every 30 min, runs at most every 3 h per machine) over the user's own connection via the Legislation tab's `legislation:search` IPC. Flow: `plan` (function returns where to start per year from `legal_feed_state` `mo_issue:<year>`, minus 6 issues, and takes a 10-min lease) → the app walks Monitorul Oficial ISSUE BY ISSUE (title search "Monitorul Oficial nr. N din" + "bis"; the service's year listing is unstable) until 3 empty numbers → ONE `submit` with the raw records → the function (JWT, `is_app_admin` only — a global feed read as the law must not accept records from any account) validates them, drops what `portal.ts` → `freeVerdict` rules out (~4 in 5; annexes folded into the act approving them), screens the rest with `claude-sonnet-5` (Haiku proved too lenient), and has `claude-opus-5` write ≤10 `legal_updates` rows per run in the background. `legal_feed_acts` records every verdict (and keeps a relevant act's text in `body` until summarised). `{dryRun:true}` answers with the decisions and writes nothing — Debug → **Legal feed: dry run**. Joint orders (one act, one record per co-signing ministry) are merged (`mergeJointActs`). An operator key (Vault `legal_feed_operator_key`, header `x-feed-key`, migration 039) lets `scripts/legal-feed-run.mjs` run the same job from any Romanian connection without the app. Rows it writes carry `origin='portal'` + `portal_id` / `act_type` / `act_number` / `act_year` / `source_url`, and the Newsletter's "Read the act →" opens that act in the Legislation tab (desktop) or on the portal. See its README.

`newsletter` — the website's newsletter sign-up, DOUBLE OPT-IN (migration 044, verify_jwt off): `subscribe` writes a pending `newsletter_subscribers` row (consent text, source and time kept as the record of consent) and emails a confirmation link (`newsletterConfirmEmail`, Resend); `confirm` / `unsubscribe` by token from `docvex.ro/newsletter.html`. Unconfirmed rows are deleted after 7 days by `run_retention_cleanup`; unsubscribing deletes the row. The footer forms (chrome.js, index.html) call it — never write newsletter rows into `enrollments` again. Any future newsletter sender must send only to `status = 'confirmed'` and put the row's `unsubscribe_token` link in every email.

`phone-upload` also has a public `sweep` action, called hourly by pg_cron through pg_net (migration 044): lapsed sessions (a day past their end) lose their objects and rows, and folders whose session row is gone are emptied.

`legal-ai` — Claude-powered Legal Newsfeed AI. Raw REST to the Anthropic Messages API, model `claude-opus-4-7` (override via `LEGAL_AI_MODEL`). `{ action: 'digest' }` returns a weekly briefing (`{ ok, summary, highImpactCount, total, generatedAt }`, or `{ ok:false, error:'ai_not_configured' }` at 200 so the client falls back); `{ action: 'ingest', items: [...] }` classifies + summarises raw legal text into `legal_updates` (service role, gated on `x-ingest-secret` matching `LEGAL_INGEST_SECRET`). Needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`.

`doc-ai` — Doc Viewer AI. Actions: `ask / summary / risks / romanian / draft / review` (Claude, `claude-opus-4-7`, override `DOC_AI_MODEL`), `ocr` (Claude, `claude-haiku-4-5`, `lib/ocr.js`), `transcribe` (OpenAI `whisper-1`, `lib/transcribe.js`; no speaker diarization — Deepgram was removed). Needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` (+ `OPENAI_API_KEY` for transcribe).

`mail-sync` / `mail-callback` — Gmail/Outlook OAuth sync for the Mail tab. `mail-sync` (JWT-gated) proxies the OAuth exchange and encrypts tokens at rest (`MAIL_TOKEN_KEY`); `mail-callback` is **public** (`verify_jwt = false`) — it bridges the provider redirect back to `docvex://` (or web `/mail`) via the `state` nonce. Needs `GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID/SECRET` and/or `MS_CLIENT_ID/SECRET`.

`project-ai` — backs the AI hub (`/ai`) and `project_ai_usage` logging. Actions `ask / suggest / generate / office`; model `claude-opus-4-7` (override `PROJECT_AI_MODEL`), with `office`/code-execution defaulting to `claude-sonnet-4-6` (`OFFICE_MODEL`). The model-picker catalog (`AI_MODELS` in `lib/projectAi.js`) now also offers `claude-opus-4-8`. Deployed as a neutral Claude.ai-style assistant (not a legal advisor); supports the real `ask_user` tool. Needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. **The file graph** (`supabase/functions/project-ai/fileGraph.ts`, actions `passport` and `crossref`, 2026-09-27 — DEPLOYED and called by the Files tab's AI scan, see lib/dataCollections. The deploy was the LIVE version plus these two actions: the older `PLACEHOLDER_RULE` was later replaced by `PLAYBOOK_RULE` — the Playbook's rules decide): the AI scan's MAP step (`{ files: [{ id, name, text, method? }] }`, ≤8 per call → one PASSPORT per file: summary, subject, document type, explicit facts, parties with kind / role / identifiers, dates, 3–8 abstract themes, the holder of an identity document; `missing` lists files the model left out) and REDUCE step (`{ passports: [...] }`, every file of the project at once, ≤400 / ~220k chars — the rest reported in `dropped` → `{ graph: { timeline, facts, links } }`, timeline and facts UNIFIED across files with `file_ids`, each link `{ from_file_id, to_file_id, connection_type, explanation, evidence, confidence }` with the type one of `CONNECTION_TYPES`: amends, supersedes, contradicts, same_party, same_subject, dependency, financial_link, evidence_for, references, chronological). STRICT JSON by a single tool whose `input_schema` is the answer, `tool_choice` pinned to it; the stable instructions are the cached system prompt, the data rides in the user turn in XML tags (`<file id name method>`, `<passports><passport id name>`) with the task restated after it; what comes back is validated server-side (`cleanPassport` / `cleanGraph`: unknown ids, self-links, types outside the enum, duplicate links — symmetric types either way round — empty values dropped, lengths capped, confidence clamped, links sorted by confidence). Model `claude-sonnet-5` (`PROJECT_AI_SCAN_MODEL` overrides; `claude-sonnet-5` and `claude-opus-5-5` added to `MODEL_ALLOW`). Verified in Node against a mocked response (schema pinning, escaping, id checks, dedup, enum filter) AND live: 5 files (lease, addendum, invoice, ID card, dispute notice) → 5 passports in 18.6 s; crossref 24.4 s → 8 timeline events, 14 facts, 13 typed links (the addendum amends the lease, the notice contradicts the invoice, the ID card and the lease are the same party by CNP). **In the scan** (lib/dataCollections): the map step is `passportBatch` (≤8 files → `passportFiles` in lib/projectAi; a passport is kept as the file's `understanding`, the old shape plus `documentType`, `parties`, `themes`; a file the model leaves out — or the whole batch when the action fails — goes through the older `understandBatch` prompt); the collections are still made by the connect / integrate prompts; then the REDUCE step `crossReference` runs over EVERY file: `graphGroups` splits the files into groups of related files (union-find over folded names and identifiers ≥5 characters; ≤40 per group — `GRAPH_GROUP`; a bigger component cut into pieces that all carry its 6 best-connected files, `GRAPH_HUBS`; small components packed first-fit), 2 calls at a time (`crossrefPassports`), each group's answer kept in the web (`web.graph.groups`, keyed by a djb2 signature of its files' paths + stamps) and REUSED while none of its files change (a rescan with nothing new = 0 calls); links merged across groups (symmetric types deduped either way round), the project-wide timeline and facts kept in `web.graph`. Each collection gets `links` (≤80, the typed links touching its files, the other end possibly in another collection; `parseCollection` reads them, `LINK_TYPES` names them) and `DataCollectionView`'s **How the files connect** shows them — the two files as chips, the type as a soft pill between them (contradicts = danger, amends / supersedes = warning, financial link = success, same party / subject = info, the rest the accent), the confidence, the explanation and the evidence — falling back to the old `connections` for a collection scanned before. The scan's progress has a `links` stage and its summary counts the links (and says when some groups failed — the next scan retries them). Verified in Node on the client path against the live function (5 passports 14.8 s; one group, 9 links 19.7 s; the unrelated car-sale offer left unlinked; the second run 0 calls, 0 ms) and `graphGroups` on 157 synthetic files (groups ≤40, every file covered, two files linked only by a CNP together, the pieces of a 100-file component sharing their hubs). `scanInternals` is exported for such checks only. NOT verified in the app itself (a real scan end to end, the view). **PICTURES ARE THEIR EXTRACTED TEXT ONLY in the scan (2026-09-27, at the user's request — the scan was far too slow):** `readFileForScan` takes the text Extract text saved for the picture, any reading of this version of the file (`extractImageText(…, { reuseAny: true })`), else reads it now in LOCAL mode (PaddleOCR in its worker, nothing sent to the AI — no strips, no whole-picture Claude transcription fallback; a picture with no text is skipped as `no_text`), and from there it is plain text like any document. The FACE STAGE no longer runs in the scan (`faceStage` / `applyFaces` kept, not called — face matches earlier scans wrote stay in the collections). **The GAUGE CLUSTER** (`components/ScanGauges.jsx` + `.css`, `sg-`): while a scan runs, HOVERING the scan button shows a card under it (portalled, no pointer, on the tooltip ground `--bg-sidebar`, hairline, kept in the window) in place of its tooltip (`ScanHover` wraps the button; idle, the ordinary Tooltip): two 240° dials — **This file** (`--info`; the file being worked on through its own steps: opening 0 → extracting its text / transcribing / reading its text 15% → read, waiting to be understood 60% → understanding its batch 80% → understood / skipped 100%; during the later stages it reads that stage's own progress and is labelled This step) and **Whole scan** (the accent; `overall`: reading + understanding 2–70% by file, connecting 72%, cross-referencing 78–95% by group, writing 97%) — each with its percentage, needle and a line under it (the step in words; files done of total), then readouts: elapsed, time left (from the pace so far, once past 4%), files per minute, analysed, skipped; the needle and arc ease over 420ms (none under reduced motion). Fed by the scan's progress events (lib/dataCollections `sayFile`: `step`, `fileFrac`, `overall`, `done`, `skipped`, `understood`; files that need no work are reported `quiet` — the gauges move, no toast), accumulated in ProjectFiles' `filesScan` (`startedAt`, `files`). Checked as a rendered card in Electron in both themes; NOT seen on a real scan. **THE SCAN BUTTON IS A CARD (2026-09-27)** (`ScanButton` in components/ScanGauges; `ScanHover` is no longer used): built as a clicked highlight is — hover = a pill (idle: what it does; scanning: the gauge cluster), CLICK = the pill morphs into a card (useMorphPill menu state, `menuHeader`, sticky) headed by the GAUGE CLUSTER (at rest: Ready, 0%), then the AI-scan head (RefPill.css's `dv-refpill`), a switch per feature (`lib/scanFeatures.js` `SCAN_FEATURES`: Documents, Pictures' text, Audio & video, Facial recognition — off by default — and Cross-reference; kept per device, `docvex:scan-features:v1`) + **Read everything again** (this scan only → `force`), and Erase memory · Close · Scan (Stop scan while running). `scanProjectFiles({ features })`: a KIND switched off is left as it is (its files keep what the web knew, nothing is removed); Facial recognition on runs `faceStage` + `applyFaces` again; Cross-reference off keeps the earlier links and graph. **Erase memory** (pressed twice; `eraseScanMemory` in lib/dataCollections, `fxEraseScanMemory` in ProjectFiles): every `.dvc` to the Trash, the web index emptied in all its homes (settings store, localStorage, `.docvex-web.json`), every file's `understanding` facet cleared — the extracted text, captions and OCR are KEPT, so the next scan only asks the AI again. **The scan cannot hang any more:** every file's reading races a time limit (`READ_LIMIT_MS`: pictures 2 min, documents 3 min, recordings 10 min) and Stop (polled every 250ms — Stop takes effect mid-file), every AI call (understand, connect / integrate, cross-reference) a limit too (`AI_LIMIT_MS`); a file that runs out of time is skipped as `timed_out`, NOT remembered (retried next scan); each file's time is logged as `[scan] <name> (<kind>): N ms` in the console; the gauge's file line counts the seconds the file has taken (`fileAt`). Checked in Electron: the card idle and running, both themes; the PaddleOCR worker and the picture pipeline under the Vite dev server (3.5 s, 6.6 s) — the cause of the reported hang was NOT reproduced. **Highlights on pictures and PDFs are CLICKED like a Word document's** (DocViewer.jsx): `refAttrsFor(hit)` is the one set of data attributes every mark carries (Word's `wrapLawRange`, a picture's word cells, a PDF's text layer), `RefCardPill({ hostRef })` gives a picture's stage and a PDF's preview the Word preview's hover pill (`refPill`) and CARD on click (Search → a new Legislation tab in the main window, Close; a click ending a text selection is ignored). A PDF's text layers are now MARKED (`PdfRefMarks` → `markPdfTextLayer`: acts, codes, CAEN, CUIs, wrapped where they lie with `dv-mark`; re-marked when a layer's text or the switch changes, via a MutationObserver) and a PDF has the **Highlights** quick action (the eye, the same device preference). NOT verified in the app: the PDF marks and both cards on real files. **THE SCAN RUNS ON WHEN THE TAB IS LEFT** (`lib/scanRunner.js`): its progress lives in a module store per project folder (`setScanState` / `useScanState(dir)` / `useAnyScanRunning`, Stop = `requestScanStop`), not in ProjectFiles' state — `fxScanFiles` fixes its folder at the start (`scanDir`) and runs to the end whether or not the page is mounted; coming back shows it where it is, and the APP SIDEBAR's Files row carries a spinner (`dot: 'spin'` → `.nav-dot.is-spin`) while any scan runs. **THE GAUGES MOVE ON THEIR OWN** (ScanGauges `Dial` + `useScanDrive`): a frame loop (no React state per frame) eases each needle toward a GOAL = the real progress, never less, + a steady fake SWEEP between the scan's events (This file: 0 → ~97% over the average time a file has taken so far, linear to 90% then slowing; Whole scan: creeping toward the next file's / stage's share, never backwards — `nextCheckpoint`) + occasional SURGES; the needle VIBRATES while a scan runs, harder the faster it climbs; the arc breathes; the stage label blinks. Drive state is per scan at module level (hover pill and card agree). No loop motion under reduced motion or a preset without `motion` (perfAllows). **FILE EXPLORER · GRAPH** (the Files toolbar's Segmented choice, `RuleOptions` `.fx-mode`, per device `docvex:files:mode:v1`; drafts tab with a folder): Graph = `components/FileGraph.jsx` (`FileGraphView`, lazy): the KNOWLEDGE GRAPH of what the scan read — `loadScanGraph` (lib/dataCollections) rebuilds `{ files, connections }` from the web index's cross-referenced groups (a scan's collections' `links` as the fallback), drawn by **vis-network** (standalone build, lazy-imported; Apache-2.0/MIT): nodes = files as rounded boxes toned by type (document accent, image info, video `--cat-update`, audio success, collection warning), edges = typed links with their label on the line, toned (contradicts danger, amends info, supersedes warning, financial link success, same party / subject category tones), arrows only on one-way types, width by confidence; colours are the THEME's tokens resolved to rgb (a probe element) and redrawn on a theme change; physics (forceAtlas2Based) runs only to lay the graph out, then `setOptions({ physics: false })` for good; the instance is destroyed on data change / unmount; hover cards (vis tooltips restyled on the tooltip ground), smooth zoom and pan, a − Fit + pill, a type legend with counts, "Only connected files", refresh; CLICKING AN EDGE opens the DRAWER (`onSelectConnection`): the type pill, both files (click opens in the Doc Viewer — `onOpenPath`), confidence bar, the AI's explanation and evidence; double-click a node opens it. Reloads when a scan finishes. The file-grid controls are hidden in Graph (`.fx-chrome-tools.is-graph`). **THE LOADERS ARE STAGED (2026-09-27, supersedes the sweep above):** every task the scan does — a file read, a batch understood, a connect chunk, a cross-reference group — runs one artificial curve (`stagedProgress` in ScanGauges): a fast BURST 0 → 40% in 0.8s, a CRUISE 40 → 80% over the task's expected time (a file: the average so far; a step: `STEP_MS`), then a HOLDING PATTERN slowing asymptotically toward 95% — never 100% by itself. Tied to the real task: when it finishes the needle SNAPS to 100% (held `SNAP_MS`) and the next task starts from 0; a skipped file drops it to 0 in the danger colour. WHOLE SCAN runs the curve over the whole run (never behind the real progress, never backwards, capped at 95%) and completes only when the scan's promise does: ProjectFiles' `finally` calls `finishScan(dir, 'ok' | 'error' | 'cancelled')` (lib/scanRunner) — the state is kept `FINISH_SHOW_MS` (2.2s) with `finished`, the card reads Done (success) / Failed / Stopped (danger), both dials at 100% or dropped back, and the sidebar's Files spinner becomes a tick (`nav-dot.is-tick`) or a red dot (`is-fail`); `isScanRunning(scan)` is what "running" means everywhere (a finished state is not). Verified in Electron with a scripted scan: 40% at 0.8s, snap to 100% on completion, burst again for the next file, drop to 0 on a skip, 100% + Done at the end. Chunked connect (546 fix): `connectChunked` (≤16 files / 24k chars per call, first chunk connects, the rest are integrated; a failed chunk retried once, then halved down to single files) — verified in Node against a stub that fails big requests. Verified in Electron (harness): the graph drawn and laid out in both themes, a real click on an edge reports its connection, the needles sweeping (39% → 88% in 3 s) and vibrating. NOT verified in the app itself. **INSIGHTS ARE PER DATA COLLECTION (2026-09-28, later the same day — supersedes where the text below puts them):** Insights left the Files toolbar's switch (File explorer · Graph only; a stored 'insights' mode falls back to the explorer) and live in each data collection's page in the Doc Viewer: `DataCollectionView` has a **Collection · Insights** Segmented choice (`docvex:collection:view:v1`), Insights rendering `CaseInsightsView` with `collection` = the `.dvc`'s path inside the project; `scopeToCollection` (lib/caseInsights) narrows the case data to that collection's source files, the collection alone, and the links / timeline among its files, so every check and every action is that collection's (a confirmed value is written into it — `writeCollection` / `revertChanges` now `notifyFilesChanged`, so the page re-reads); the kind of case and the signatures report are kept per collection (`<dir>#<rel>`). **Contradictions know about duplicates:** `copyGroups(data, texts, dupes)` groups the files that are copies of one document — the name with its copy marks off ("(edited)", "(2)", "(scan)", "- Copy"), the same size and type, texts ≥ 80% the same words, plus identical / near-identical files once the Duplicates section has run (its answer is handed back, `onFound`); a contradiction whose files are all copies of one document is `copiesOnly` (not counted, folded under "N differences between copies of the same file", each reading still confirmable), one with copies on both sides says part of it is a misreading, and every version counts its `documents`. **MRZ read by the AI:** `MRZ_PROMPT` (lib/roIdDocuments — the parser-component prompt: surname<<given<names, sex after the birth date, YYMMDD dates with the century pivot at the current year, expiry 20YY, ROU) is part of the autofill's `ro_id` (`mrz` key) and of the fallback understanding prompt (`idDocument.mrz`), and `lib/mrzAi.js` `readMrzWithAi` parses a strip in the scan when the local reader found none or its check digits failed (`hasMrzLines`); `normalizeRoId` then takes the AI's strip (`normalizeMrzReading`, sources `mrz-ai`) to fill gaps and compare with the printed side, with a warning that its check digits could not be verified. Verified in Node (copy grouping, contradictions with copies, scoping, the AI strip filling a noisy card); NOT in the app. **INSIGHTS (2026-09-28)** — the Files toolbar's switch is now File explorer · Graph · **Insights** (`components/CaseInsights.jsx` + `.css`, `ci-`, lazy; logic in **`lib/caseInsights.js`**, which reads the scan's web index through `loadScanIndex` (lib/dataCollections) and each scanned file's text through `textOfFile` — saved text or local extraction, NEVER a paid OCR). One section at a time (a Segmented choice). (The **Ask** section was REMOVED on 2026-09-28 and its features moved to the ADVISOR tab — see `lib/aiSources.js` in the Advisor paragraph; `askCase` stays in lib/caseInsights because Contradictions' Suggest still uses it.) **Contradictions** (`collectClaims` → `findContradictions`: per person / company — parties keyed by `foldName`, a fact attributed by the role or name it mentions, else the ID document's holder, else the file's only person — CNP, date of birth, address (same place written differently is NOT flagged, `sameAddress`) and, per matter (the collection's title, else the subject's head), amounts by label and signature dates; two files needed; files that amend / supersede each other are noted as possibly intended; plus the scan's own `contradicts` links; each is EXPLAINED in plain words (`explainLocal`: a headline — "The files give X 2 different CNPs" — and why it matters for that kind of detail, e.g. a CNP failing its check digit is certainly mistyped) and shown as VERSIONS side by side (`.ci-compare`: Version A / B, the value with what differs from the others highlighted — `DiffText`, characters, or words for an address — and every file saying it with its document type and its own wording, `said` on each value from `findContradictions`); an AI one as File A / File B with what each says and "What the AI noticed"; each is RESOLVED in place: a local one by **This is right** on a value (`resolveContradiction` → the Data collections holding its files — for a person's detail, those named after the person — get a "<kind> (confirmed)" fact and, for CNP / date of birth / address, the record field) or **Both are right**; an AI one by **<file> is right** (a "Contradiction resolved" fact) or **Not a contradiction**; **Suggest** asks the question box which is right (PAID). Decisions are kept with the project (settings store `insight-resolutions`, localStorage fallback `docvex:insight-resolutions:v1:<dir>`) under the contradiction's id + a signature of its values — a new value reopens it; resolved ones fold under "N resolved" with **Undo** (the collections written back byte for byte, `unresolveContradiction`); the section's count is the open ones. Verified in Node (write, undo, reopen) and in the Insights harness (resolve → count down, Undo → back).) · **Authenticity** (`checkAuthenticity`: `checkCnp` — check digit 279146358279, a real date, county 01–52/70 — against the birth dates the files give; `parseMrz` — ICAO TD1 / TD2 (the older Romanian card, whose optional field is the CNP's first digit + last six) / TD3, every check digit, then against the printed side: number (series and number printed apart), surname, CNP birth date and sex; expiry from the strip, a validity fact or the card's own "Valabilitate" line; every CUI's check digit (`findCuis` keeps the failing ones), and **Check with ANAF** (`checkCuisWithAnaf`: missing, inactive, struck off, named differently)) · **Missing documents** (`CASE_TYPES`: property sale, inheritance, company setup, lease, vehicle sale, civil lawsuit — each item a folded regex over name + document type + subject + summary; the kind guessed from what matches, overridable, kept per folder `docvex:insights:case:v1:<dir>`) · **Duplicates** (on request, local: same size → SHA-256; pictures by a 64-bit difference hash of the OS thumbnail (`?thumb=128`), Hamming ≤ 6; texts by 5-word shingles, Jaccard ≥ 0.6, older → newer by modified time) · **Signatures & stamps** (on request, PAID: `compareSignatures` — pictures and PDF pages (first + last, or all of a 1–2 page file, rendered by `pdfPageImage`) whose reading suggests a signed act, ≤30, 5 per call: every mark described with its owner; then per owner + kind in two files or more the pictures go again side by side, `{ consistent, outliers, note }`; `usageAction: 'insights-signatures'`; the report kept per folder, `docvex:insights:signatures:v1:<dir>`) · **Plates & places** (`findPlates`: Romanian plates — county code or B, digits, three letters — in the files' text and facts, with the people, collections and dates around them; `readPhotoPlaces`: the first 256 KB of every JPEG by a Range request, `readExif` / `gpsDecimal` now exported from lib/fileMetadata — date taken and GPS, the timeline's events of the same day, photos within 200 m, Open map). The GRAPH has a lens switch, **Files · People & things** (`docvex:graph:lens:v1`): `buildEntityGraph` — people (merged by CNP), companies, institutions, properties (facts naming a property, keyed by cadastral number when there is one) and vehicles (plates) as nodes; a person's role in a company (`represents`), a party's role toward a property / vehicle (labelled with the role), people named in one document (`same_document`, documents of ≤6 parties); FileGraph takes `onSelectNode` (a drawer: what it is, what it is tied to, the files it is named in) and an edge's `label` / `sources`. Verified in Node (CNP, MRZ TD1/TD2/TD3 incl. a correctly built Romanian strip, plates, dates, amounts, contradictions, checklist, entity graph) and in an Electron harness with a stubbed project (every section drawn in both themes, Ask answered with source chips, the People & things graph laid out). NOT verified in the app on a real scanned project; the AI calls (Ask, signatures), ANAF, duplicates and EXIF were not run against real files. Identical files in Duplicates have **Merge** (pick the copy to keep; the others go to the Trash, pressed twice). A **Legal history** section lists each document read in its era (see lib/legalHistory below) with a land-measure converter. (The Files tab's hand-made **collections of files** — exact copies bundled as one item, `lib/fileCollections` — were REMOVED at the user's request on 2026-09-28; Data collections are unaffected. `lib/fileSimilarity.js` keeps the helpers the Insights duplicates use. A leftover `.docvex/settings/file-collections.json` in a project is simply ignored.) **ROMANIAN IDENTITY DOCUMENTS (`lib/roIdDocuments.js`)** — one normalised reading for CEI (TD1 strip), CI / CIP (1997–, TD2), passports (TD3), the BI booklet (`BI_CEAUSESCU`, 1949–1997, CNP only from 1978) and pre-1949 papers (`BI_INTERBELIC`): `{ document_type, document_series, document_number, passport_number, cnp, last_name, first_names[], nationality, place_of_birth, gender M/F, birth_date (YYYY-MM-DD), issue_date / expiry_date (DD.MM.YYYY), issuing_authority, address { county, city, street, number, building, entrance, floor, apartment, raw }, parents_names, physical_description, viza_flotant[] }` + `cnp_info`, `mrz`, `expired`, `warnings`, `sources` (field → ai / text / mrz / cnp). `decodeCnp` (sex/century incl. 7/8 foreign residents with an inferred century and 9 foreign citizens, birth date, county incl. 70 = central code, check digit), `parseMrz` (all three formats, every check digit), `parseRoIdText` (the card's trilingual labels, value on the line or the next; validity ranges; SERIA/NR; parents from "Fiul lui X și al Y"; viză de flotant lines), `parseAddress` (jud./plate codes → county names, mun./oraș/com., street types, nr./bl./sc./et./ap., București sectors), `normalizeGender` (Bărbătesc / Femeiesc / CNP digit…), `roRx` (a pattern written without diacritics matches Î Â Ș Ț Ă, their plain OCR misreadings and the cedilla forms; `\b` made letter-aware). `normalizeRoId(ai, text)` cross-checks the AI's answer, the labels, the strip and the CNP and records every disagreement. Wired into the identity autofill (`identityExtract` asks the AI for `ro_id` — `RO_ID_PROMPT` — then `completePerson` fills missing record fields, `roIdToRecordFields`, and returns `roId`) and into the AI scan (an identity document's understanding carries `roId`; a person's collection takes it into its record, empty fields only). Insights' CNP / strip checks now use it. Verified in Node (CNP variants, strip TD2, an old CI's text, a booklet, an interwar paper, a passport, disagreement warnings). **ADDRESSES WRITTEN ANY WAY (lib/roIdDocuments):** `sameAddress(a, b)` compares addresses by their PARTS (`parseAddress` — street types, `nr.` / `numărul`, `bloc` / `bl.`, `scara`, `etaj`, `ap.` written out or abbreviated, glued or spaced, a blank `-` no value; bare trailing places "…, CONSTANTA, CONSTANTA" read as locality then county; a county code → its name, `Jud.CT` = Constanța; a house number of ≤3 digits right after the street name split off, so "Răscoalei 1907" keeps its 1907): number / block / entrance / floor / apartment equal when both give them, county and locality the same place (either may be the other's), the street sharing its name words — so "Strada STR. RASCOALEI 1907, nr. 59, bloc -, … CONSTANTA, CONSTANTA" and "Jud.CT Mun.Constanța, Str.1907 nr.59" are one address. `mergeAddresses(values)` makes ONE fuller address out of equal ones ("Str. Rascoalei 1907 nr. 59, Constanța, Jud. Constanța"): each part from the source that gives it best (a value over a blank, the fuller street name, the spelling with diacritics, a county's name over its code, ALL-CAPS in ordinary case). Used by Insights (an address version shows the merged address; each file's own wording under it) and by the scan (a person's record address made fuller by a new spelling of it). **OFFICIAL DOCUMENTS WIN (`lib/docAuthority.js`):** `documentAuthority({ name, u })` ranks a file — 5 identity document (CI / CEI / buletin, passport, licence, residence permit, civil-status certificate), 4 state record (ONRC / trade register, ANAF, ANCPI / land register, a ministry, Monitorul Oficial, a court decision, town hall, police, prefecture, SPCLEP), 3 notarised act, 2 signed document, 1 anything else. It decides: `mergeAddresses` (values may carry `authority`: the more official source wins a real disagreement, a fuller form of the same part still completes it), the scan's person records (`fillRecord(c, rec, { authority, rel })` — a field filled from a less official document is REPLACED by a more official one; `recordSources` on the collection, read back by `parseCollection`, remembers each field's source), the AI prompts (`AUTHORITY_RULE` in the scan's RECORD_RULE, the identity autofill, the question box — each file tagged `official="…"` — and Suggest, which names each side's rank and says to favour the official document; for an address, the identity document or state record states the legal address), and Insights' Contradictions (versions ranked most official first, labelled with the rank, the top one marked **Most official source**; the AI's contradictions show each file's rank too). Verified in Node (7 address cases, two merges, ranks, an ID card's block beating a contract's). **LEGAL HISTORY (`lib/legalHistory.js`)** — every text read in the law of its time, locally: `detectLegalEra` (six eras 1500–today, `LEGAL_ERAS` with the codes that applied; from the document's latest DATED year incl. Byzantine "leat 7250", the script — Cyrillic / transitional / Latin — and weighted era vocabulary; words chosen not to catch modern ones: CAP only dotted, no "vecini"/"dotat"/"birou"/"Iași"), `LEXICON` (≈40 archaic terms → the modern concept + implication, in Romanian; `findLegalTerms`, `normalizeLegalText` glosses them in one pass), `findPropertyDecrees` (`PROPERTY_DECREES`: Legea 187/1945, Decretele 83/1949, 92/1950, 111/1951, 115/1959, 218/1960, 712/1966, 223/1974, Legile 58/1974 & 59/1974, with the restitution law; any decree of 1945–1989), `findPropertyRisks` (DOTAL_RESTRICTION, SOCIALIST_USE_ONLY, UNREGULATED_PREEMPTION_PROTIMISIS, EXPROPRIATION_DECREE, COOPERATIVIZED_LAND, LAND_ALIENATION_BAN_1974, UNAUTHENTICATED_TITLE, EMPHYTEUSIS, UNREGISTERED_OLD_REGISTRY, CO_OWNERSHIP_DEVALMASIE — each with an explanation for the lawyer), `convertHistoricalLandToMetric` / `findLandMeasures` (pogon 5 011,79 m²; fălcie 14 321,95; prăjină 179,02 Moldova / 34,80 Țara Românească; stânjen pătrat 3,8671 / 4,9729 / 3,5966 Transilvania; jugăr cadastral 5 754,64; jugăr unguresc 4 315,98; lanț read as the cadastral jugăr, flagged; region from the text or given; parts summed — "2 pogoane și 40 stânjeni"; stânjeni "de moșie / în lățime" are a width and are not converted), `analyzeLegalHistory` (the `historical_legal_analysis` block), `legalHistoryNote` (for prompts), `compactLegalHistory` (kept). Wired into: the AI scan (a historical file goes to the AI with the note prefixed, and its understanding keeps `legalHistory`), the project Advisor's digest (era, risks, decrees, surfaces; and the ID reading), the Doc Viewer advisor (a HISTORICAL LEGAL CONTEXT block for the open file) and Insights (Legal history). Verified in Node on sample texts of each era and in the Insights harness; NOT against real archive documents.

`legal-assist` — **deployed-only, no source under `supabase/functions/`** (it lives with the Word add-in in `docs/word-addin/`). Claude backend for the **DocVex Legal AI Microsoft Word add-in** (Office.js taskpane); JWT-gated, tasks `summary / risks / romanian / ask`; model `claude-opus-4-7` (override `LEGAL_ASSIST_MODEL` / `LEGAL_AI_MODEL`). Needs `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY`. This is a separate product surface from the in-app Doc Viewer — don't confuse it with `doc-ai`.

## Project data (files, index, knowledge)

The contract is `src/projectIndex/README.md` — read it before touching any of this.

- **Project file** `<Project name>.docvex` at the case folder's root (JSON: project id, name, version). Linking a folder writes it; double-clicking it in Explorer opens DocVex on that project (`.docvex` registered per install, 15 s after start; `project:opened` → `ProjectFileOpened` in App.jsx selects it and goes to /files). The Files grid hides it.
- **Machine index** `<userData>/project-index/<projectId>.db` (built-in `node:sqlite`, WAL; `registry.json` maps project → folder). A CACHE: listing comes from it instantly (`projectFiles`), a background reconcile walks the disk after opening, and a recursive watcher per open project (≤4, LRU) stats only changed paths. Every change reaches every window as ONE `project:delta`; windows never re-list. Measured on the packaged build, 3,000 files: the index listing 33 ms vs 216–230 ms for the old full walk (SSD; the gap grows on HDD / network shares).
- **File identity**: portable uuids in `.docvex/ids.json`, tracked locally by the NTFS file id (`stat({ bigint }).ino` — survives rename, move and edit; Word's save-by-rename keeps the id). Other machines match by path + size + mtime, then hash, then path.
- **Knowledge** (AI facets, metadata, captions, OCR snippets, descriptions, audio envelopes, Word themes; `faces` is LOCAL-only) lives in the index and, portably, in `.docvex/knowledge/<ab>/<sha256>.dvxe` keyed by CONTENT hash. Project settings (scan tags, folder colours, the web index) in `.docvex/settings/<store>.dvxe`.
- **Folder encryption (2026-09-28)**: everything written into `.docvex/knowledge` and `.docvex/settings` is SEALED with the PROJECT's folder key (`src/projectIndex/folderSeal.js`: AES-256-GCM, `{ v: 2, alg: 'A256GCM', data }` under the `.dvxe` extension, so older builds — which read `.json` only — never overwrite it). The key is per project, made and kept by the server (`project_folder_keys`, migration 045) and handed only to members by `get_project_folder_key`; the renderer fetches it (`lib/projectFolderKey.js` `ensureFolderKey`, called when a project's folder is opened or located) and gives it to the index (`projectFolderKey` / `project:folder-key`), which keeps it in the project's database (sealed with the index key) for offline use and then SEALS THE FOLDER in the background (`sealFolder`: plain shards / settings rewritten sealed, what only the index knew written out). Without the key NOTHING new goes into the folder (the index keeps it; a removal still reaches a plain shard). Plain `.json` from older builds is still read. Account sync's data bundles (`.docvex-data.json`, the private bundle) are sealed with the same key (WebCrypto, same format); a sealed bundle that can't be opened aborts the sync rather than being overwritten. Tests: `tests/projectIndex/index.test.mjs` ("folder key…"). Per-user private data (advisor threads, conversations) in the index's `private` table — never in `.docvex/`.
- **Renderer**: `lib/projectIndexClient.js` wraps the IPC (`KNOWLEDGE_KINDS`, `SETTINGS_STORES` are the one list), hydrates an in-memory copy per project / file so the stores' SYNCHRONOUS APIs (getAiFacet…) keep working, writes in the background, falls back to localStorage while the index isn't answering. The old localStorage data is migrated once per project, then removed.
- **Sync**: `.docvex/` travels with the case folder (OneDrive / Dropbox) AND through account sync (`projectSync.js` lists and syncs `ids.json`, `knowledge/**`, `settings/**`; after pulling, `projectReconcile` re-reads ids + settings and the re-hydrate imports shards).
- **Files tab**: `ProjectFiles.jsx` keeps an in-memory index from `projectFiles` + deltas (buffered by `rev` during load); the optimistic overlay settles when the delta shows the result (4 s fallback). `FilesWorkspace`'s `VirtualCells` renders only rows near the viewport (flat, list, grouped and name-search views; same markup; measured row heights, keyboard / marquee / rename work on items not drawn).
- Mutations still go through the `local-folder:*` IPC — the watcher turns them into deltas.

## Notification system

Three layers:

1. **Source hooks** (`src/notifications/sources/use*NotificationSource.js`)
   - `useAuthNotificationSource(notify, { ready })` — SIGNED_IN welcome, SIGNED_OUT goodbye.
   - `useUpdateNotificationSource(notify, { ready })` — installer state transitions (downloading / downloaded / restart).
   - `useSocialNotificationSource(notify, userId, { ready })` — placeholder hook for future @-mentions / DMs.
2. **Context** — `notify(payload)` accepts `{ category, variant, title, body, icon?, priority?, duration?, persistent?, dedupeKey?, osLevel? }`. Resolves dedupe strategy (`coalesce` / `replace` / `insert`), enforces the 3-toast cap, mirrors writes to Supabase + localStorage. Returns the notification id (existing id on coalesce).
3. **Action registry** (`src/notifications/actionRegistry.js`) — maps notification types to icons / actions / titles for the history view + test menu.

UI: `NotificationToast` auto-dismisses after `duration` (5 s default; `persistent: true` opts out), `NotificationCenter` is both the floating toast stack AND the `/notifications` page with category / priority filters. Icons live in `src/notifications/icons.jsx`; the dev "Send all test notifications" menu fires every entry in `src/notifications/testNotifications.js`.

**Realtime flow.** notify() mutates local state immediately, then asyncly mirrors to Supabase + localStorage. Realtime INSERT events from other devices dedupe by `id` (no re-toast). UPDATEs sync `read_at` across devices; DELETEs remove rows.

## Pages

> **2026-09-28 — tabs changed.** The **Advisor** (`/ai`, pages/Projects/ProjectAI + ProjectAITools, lib/aiSources, lib/advisorPrompts, the Advisor's chat store instance) and the **Timeline** (`/events`, pages/Projects/ProjectEvents, lib/timelineCouncil, components/CouncilSphere) tabs were REMOVED; `/ai` redirects to `/research`, `/events` to `/network` (lib/caseTimeline stays — the digest still reads a saved timeline; lib/advisorChats keeps only the `createChatStore` factory Research uses). A **Neural network** tab (`/network`, pages/Projects/ProjectNetwork, project section of the sidebar) now holds the knowledge graph (components/FileGraph) that was the Files tab's Graph view — Files is the file explorer only. **Research** is its own card ABOVE the app sidebar, apart from it (`.sidebar.sidebar-research`: drawn exactly as the app sidebar — its ground, border, corners, shadow — apart from it by a gap; the rail starts under it via `--research-card-h`), and has an **i** right of its search opening `components/SourcesModal` — every connected platform in its colour with what it holds and how it is reached (free / paid / bundled), the AI, and the platforms not connected yet (data: `lib/legalSources.js`, which the Legislation placeholder pages read too). The **Legislation** tab's list starts with a fixed **Platforms** group (components/LegalBrowser `PlatformItems`): the **Newsletter first** (it is no longer a sidebar entry — its "new brief" pill is on the Legislation row, and pages/Newsletter renders inside `LegalWorkspace`), then one item per platform — a connected one opens the one search with its prefix typed (`lege ` / `caen ` / `dosar ` / `cui `), the Newsletter and a planned source open their own page in a tab (`showRoot` / `isRootOn` in lib/legalBrowser; such root tabs are not listed among the open tabs; the rail is always drawn). **COLLECTIONS OF FILES (2026-09-28, `lib/fileGroups.js`, components/FileGroupView):** a collection is a CUSTOM FOLDER pointing at files anywhere in the project (paths inside it; the files never move) — NOT the AI scan's Data collections (`.dvc`). Stored in the project settings store `collections` (`SETTINGS_STORES.fileGroups`, travels with the folder) + a localStorage mirror. Shown at the project's ROOT after the Trash; its TILE is a FIXED square of 2×2 thumbnails of its first four files (their type icons when there is no preview; empty slots faint — `CollectionMosaic`, `.fx-coll-mosaic`, side = the thumb box's height; `item.previewItems` from ProjectFiles), the list view keeping the stacked-cards `CollectionGlyph` (`item.collectionEntry`); its menu: Open / Rename / Delete collection (the files stay); dropping files on it adds them. The Files FOOTER's **Collect** button (`onCollect` → ProjectFiles `fxCollect`): with items selected, one collection of them (a folder brings everything under it); with nothing selected, DocVex groups ONLY files that are ONE THING (`findSamePairs`, every signal local): **EXACT DUPLICATES** (same size, then SHA-256); **SAME CONTENT** in another file or format (a Word file and its PDF, a photo and its scan — the TEXT read from both, 5-word shingles, Jaccard ≥ `CONTENT_MIN` 0.72; pictures also by the thumbnail's difference hash, Hamming ≤ 6, unless their texts say different pages); **SAME DOCUMENT** in parts — an identity card's front and back (the same valid CNP, the same series + number printed and/or in the MRZ, or the same holder — name words sorted — born the same day), pages that say so ("Pagina 2 din 5" beside "4 din 5" with the same document type / identifiers / name stem / hour), names differing only by side or page (`partOfName`: fata/verso/front/back/p2/page 3 — never bare numbers, IMG_1 ≠ IMG_2), a PDF's "(pages)" folder beside the PDF, photos of the same kind of document naming the same people taken ≤20 min apart. Text = saved extracted text (lib/aiData `bestTextFor`), else read now from Word / PDF / text (≤15 MB, cached per session — ProjectFiles `groupTextOf`), plus the scan's `understanding` (document type, parties' identifiers, the identity document). Links between merely related files group NOTHING any more. Collections are named by the scan's document type + holder / subject, else "Copies of …" / "… (all formats)". Opening one (crumb Home › name; the page's MASTHEAD — the header a folder gets — takes its name as the title and what is in it as the line under it, eyebrow "Project files · Home › Collection") is drawn BY THE FILES GRID ITSELF — its files are the grid's own ITEMS (ProjectFiles `collectionItems`, one per section, id `<d|l|f>:<id>`), so click, double-click, the right-click menu (plus **Remove from collection**, bulk-aware — `onRemoveFromCollection`), multi-select, the rubber band, drag and drop, the keys, rename and delete behave exactly as anywhere in Files — laid out by `collectionLayout` in the category view's sections (`.fx-cat-section` / `.fx-cat-head`), ONE PER KIND — **Exact duplicates** · **Same content, other format** · **Same document** (`SAME_KINDS`) — every group a plain block under the divider (`.fgv-cluster`, no frame or ground), its pill (`.fgv-tie`: Exact copy · Same content · Same document) ON THE SECTION'S DIVIDER at its far right, the hairline running from the count into it and centred on it (`sec.tie`, `order: 1` after `.fx-cat-head::after`), and each tile's hover saying why (the pair's reason), then **Other files** (`clustersOf(rels, { pairs })` → `{ sections, loose, why }`; the pairs it was MADE with are stored on it (`pairs`, so it shows its sections at once) and re-checked when it opens and again once the file listing has loaded — `groupGraph`, never left empty by a listing that hadn't arrived). Verified in Node (copy marks, duplicate sets, proposals, clusters); NOT in the app. **LEGISLATION, APP-WIDE (2026-09-28 — the app's main feature):** ONE behaviour everywhere — every reference to Romanian law in ANY text of a window (acts, codes, CAEN codes, court files, CUIs; lib/lawRefs `findFollowableRefs`) is HIGHLIGHTED in its platform's colour, HOVERING shows the highlight tooltip (components/RefHitPill), a CLICK opens the SIDE DRAWER from the right with the record (the act, CAEN code, court file, company). `lib/lawDetect.js`: the CSS Custom Highlight API (`::highlight(lawref-<kind>)`, components/LawDetect.css) — Ranges only, nothing written into the DOM React owns; a MutationObserver re-reads only what changed, on idle; lib/lawRefs lazy-loaded; `hitAt(x, y)` the hit test (caret position + the range's rects). SKIPPED: fields, code, links and buttons, text already marked by its own renderer (the Word preview, PDF / picture text layers, `.lg-ref`, `.ai-lref`), tooltips, the sidebar, the title bar, `[data-no-lawdetect]`. `lib/lawDrawer.js`: `openLawRef(hit)` — the app drawer (pages/ResearchDrawer's views, which now carry their stylesheets), one view deeper while open; a page with a drawer of its own takes the press (`setLawRefOpener` — Research does). `components/LawDetect` (mounted in AppShell and the Doc Viewer) runs the layer, the hover pill (on lib/pointer) and the drawer. The Doc Viewer's own marks (Word, pictures, PDFs) and its AI answers now open the same drawer (`openMarkRef`, `openAnswerRef`) instead of the card / the main window; SideDrawer does not close on a press that lands on a reference. `viewForRef` moved to lib/portalRecords. The Legislation tab's own act marks keep following citations inside the tab. NOT verified in the app. **Research no longer scrolls when everything fits**: the shell / column min-heights are `--aifit-h` (what is left under the shell), not the sticky list's height. **AI answers (2026-09-28, later):** `components/AiAnswer` is ONE reveal for streamed and replayed answers — the typewriter chases the stream at an even pace (≥90 chars/s, a backlog eaten 1.5×/s) and a `revealKey` shared by the stream bubble and the final message carries the typing across (no jump, no retype); legislation is marked IN THE RENDER (a rehype pass, `data-ref-hit`), so the marks appear while it types; `AiRefPill({ hostRef })` gives Research's thread the Legislation tab's hover pill over them (components/RefHitPill), a click opening Research's drawer. **Research's chat rows are colour-coded** (lib/researchChats `describe`, via createChatStore's `describe` option): a chat the AI answered in wears the ACCENT; a chat holding a DIRECT search is drawn as a Legislation tab from its latest one, and STAYS so while later questions name no other item (an identifier the search did not hold — number/year, file number, CUI, CAEN — turns it into an AI conversation) — the platform's address in its colour · the item's kind, the item's name as title, the Legislation hover pill — in the app sidebar and the page's rail. **Pill rule** (Design system → Pills, "Choice pill — the two-line rule"): a `.ai-choice` on one line is fully rounded, on two lines or more half the radius (`useMultilinePills` → `.is-multiline`, measured); pills keep their own heights, centred on their row. NOT verified in the app.
