| `Legislation` (`/legislation`) | **One sidebar entry, three tabs** — `/newsletter`, `/legislation` and `/caen` each keep their own route (the Doc Viewer and the Newsletter deep-link into them) and their own masthead (`compact={false}` — the tab bar is the mini header), and each draws `components/LegalTabs` under it: the Activity tab's sticky 40px toolbar holding a `FilterTabs` strip, tabs named for the SOURCE each one reads — (the Newsletter is NO LONGER one of them: it is a sidebar entry of its own, above Legislation, carrying the "new brief" pill, and draws LegalTabs with `standalone` — the bar's second line alone, search + History, no tabs row) · "legislatie.just.ro" · "insse.ro" · "portal.just.ro" · "anaf.ro" · then the placeholders "termene.ro - listafirme.ro" · "bpi.ro" · "ancpi.ro" · "rejust.ro" · "unbr.ro" · "eur-lex.europa.eu"; what each one is for is its masthead's title, and the masthead eyebrow repeats the site as "source: …" — with a SECOND LINE under the hairline holding the page's own view switch at the left (`tools`, when it has one) and the page's SEARCH at the right (`search` prop: value / onChange / placeholder / onSubmit; the Files search to the letter, Ctrl+F focuses it; pages with nothing to search leave it out and it shows disabled). The scroller's top padding is zeroed under the bar and carried on the masthead, as Activity does, so the bar pins and frosts at the right place; the bar remembers the tab it was on (module-level `lastActive`) so on the next page it mounts with the underline sliding from there (`FilterTabs` gained `fromId`) and the content under it enters from that side, as Activity's feed does (`.lgt-bar.is-enter-*` ~ siblings); the masthead's own divider is dropped (the bar's hairline runs through the middle of the active tab's underline). All three pages cap at `--content-max-width` like Versions. **Every live source has a DICE** in the bar's second line (`DiceGlyph` / `.lgt-dice` in LegalTabs): a random entry THAT EXISTS — Legislation draws a year and a common title word and fills the form with one of the portal's answers (kind, number, year — it does not search or open it); CAEN a random class of the revision on show (the list opens its card, the picker walks to it); Court files draws a court and a recent working day, asks for that day's docket and opens one of its files; ANAF draws a hundred well-formed CUIs (valid check digit: the other digits × 7 5 3 2 1 7 5 3 2, × 10, mod 11) in one request and keeps one that exists. Each redraws a few times before giving up with a note. **Two more tabs are live** — `portal.just.ro` (`pages/PortalJust`, court files; its whole search is the Legislation bar in the tab bar's second line (`tools`, `components/LegalBar`): the dice, the Case files / A court's day choice as the bar's first DROPDOWN (`BarPicker`; `BarSwitch`, segments, stays in the component for a two-way choice elsewhere), then per mode the number, the object, the court (`BarPicker` over the courts grouped by kind, a narrowing box at the head of the list, "Any court" first; a group whose entries all open with the same words — Curtea de Apel, Tribunalul, Judecătoria — is headed by those words as a STICKY head and its rows show only what follows them; the entry chosen is a plain row like the rest — picked again it just closes — marked by a filled accent circle at its right end (`.lg-menu-mark`; it is no longer faded or inert) — the words MOST entries share, two in three at least, so "Curtea Militară de Apel" among the courts of appeal keeps the head at "Curtea de Apel" and shows its whole name), the period as two date fields, Search — or the court, the day, List the hearings; the party is the words box (cases mode only). No form in the page body any more like the CAEN one) and `anaf.ro` (`pages/Anaf`, company records; the CUI — one, or a pasted list, whose line breaks the words box turns into spaces on paste (`search.onPaste` in LegalTabs) — is the words box, and the bar holds the dice, a still count (`BarNote`) and Look up; no form in the page body), see `lib/courts.js` / `lib/anaf.js`; desktop only, like Legislation. The six placeholder routes render ONE component, so their element is keyed by tab in AppRoutes — otherwise going placeholder → placeholder reused the mounted page and the bar's enter motion (played on mount) never ran. **Six tabs are placeholders** (`stub: true` in `LEGAL_TABS`; `pages/LegalSourceStub` serves them all, keyed by pathname — `/firme`, `/bpi`, `/ancpi`, `/rejust`, `/unbr`, `/eurlex`): a masthead, the bar, and an "Unavailable at the moment" state drawn as the Doc Viewer advisor's empty state (a bare thin-stroke unplug mark, the title, muted text — centred, no frame, 64ch): first what the page will do in plain words (`plain`), then, past a hairline, a fold ("How it would connect") holding the technical access line and the uses as a row of pills. Connecting one = a real page on its route + dropping the flag. **Every tab REMEMBERS what was on it** (`lib/pageMemory.js`: `recallPage(key)` / `usePageMemory(key, state, rootRef)` — an in-memory map, per page key, of the state that matters and the scroller's scroll position, taken as the page unmounts and put back two frames after the next mount; in memory only, a restart starts clean): the Legislation tab's form, results, open act, find and sync state; Court files' mode, form, results, docket and open file; ANAF's CUI and answer; CAEN's words, picker preview and the `?code=` / `?rev=` the route carried (a route change drops them, so they are put back on the first render); the Newsletter's filters and search. A page reads `recallPage` as its `useState` initial values and hands `usePageMemory` a memoised snapshot. Every one of these routes is in `FLUSH_CONTENT_ROUTES` (AppShell) and `CHROMELESS_FULLSCREEN_ROUTES` (SplitView) — add new ones to both. The sidebar item is active on all of them (`activeOn` in Sidebar's `renderNavItem`). (A single shared masthead over the tabs was tried and reverted.) The national legislative portal, in the app — search the Ministry of Justice's own web service, read an act laid out as a document in DocVex's typography, and keep it on this machine so it is still there when the portal is not. Personal route, public like the Newsletter (the law is not project data). Desktop only: the service refuses browser requests and the archive is a folder on disk. Model + offline fallback in `lib/legislation.js`; transport and archive in main.js. **The page:** the search is the TAB BAR's box, in PLAIN TEXT: any words go to the portal as TITLE words first, then as words anywhere in the TEXT, and — both empty, signed in — the project AI is asked what a Romanian act about that is called (up to three phrases in the law's own vocabulary, `suggestTerms` → `askProjectAi`, sonnet) and each is tried both ways (`searchLegislationPlain` in the lib; `mode` / `terms` come back and the strip says "Found in the text…" / "Found by asking for …" / "Also tried …"); at the LEFT of the tab bar's second line (the `tools` slot) one BAR — **`components/LegalBar`**, the SHARED recipe (`LegalBar` form + `BarDice` / `BarSwitch` / `BarPicker` / `BarInput` (num · text · wide · date) / `BarNote` / `BarGo`; `LegalBar.css` holds every `.lg-bar` / `.lg-input` / `.lg-kind` / `.lg-menu` / `.lg-seg` / `.lg-random` / `.lg-go` rule) — **the dropdown (`BarPicker`) is built exactly as the calendar pickers are** (the calendar's base): TWO SECTIONS, the field (its edge accent while open — in a joined bar an inset line, so nothing moves — keeping its hover wash) and the list, a box of its own 6px under it (`MENU_GAP`), one accent edge all round, four equal rounded corners, no shadow, fitted to the window once drawn (pulled back inside; above the field when there is no room below, else raised; a solo picker lines up with the field's right edge), FADING in and out (`.lg-menu.is-placed` / `.is-closing`, `FADE_MS` = 150, taken away after its fade; a scroll / resize / another dropdown opening / reduced motion close at once) that Court files and ANAF draw their fields with too, so the three tabs' search options and switches are one thing — the dice (accent, the Search button's twin), Kind (`BarPicker`: the app's own dropdown, its field as WIDE AS ITS CHOICE — the label measured off a hidden copy (`.lg-kind-measure`) plus the field's padding, border, gap and chevron, clamped 56–460px — the copy is `width: max-content`, since inside the field it was capped at the field's own width and a longer label could never grow it — re-measured whenever the label's real width changes too (a ResizeObserver: the font arriving late, the field drawn larger), and GLIDING to the new width on a change (a CSS width transition on `.lg-kind`; the first width is set without motion); its open list is as wide as its longest entry (`width: max-content`, never narrower than the field, within the window) — every picker in every tab — a native select takes neither the bar's frost nor a rounded foot — a list portalled under the field on the bar's ground, bottom corners rounded, the chevron turning over as it opens), Number, Year, Search, joined by hairlines and drawn as the words box beside it, no labels; focus is an inset outline so nothing in the row moves (`.lg-bar`, a `<form>`) — which narrows any search, filled by the DICE (`randomAct`: a year and a common title word drawn, the portal asked, and one of its answers written INTO THE FORM — kind, number, year — and nothing else: no search run, no act opened, the list untouched; Search is the user's press); "Clear" (forget every kept act) sits at the top right of Recently viewed as a sidebar tab button (`.lgt-tool-btn`); the dice lists nothing (it only fills the form); no card fills on the page; an act spans the full width. A FIXED search — kind, number AND year — opens its act at once instead of listing it (one act, or the versions of one: the newest in force; `runNow`). Where what is on show came from — "Live from the portal" / "From your copy on this machine" (the open act's own source, `actSource`, else the last search's) — is a FILLED pill-BUTTON (success / warning ground, an external-link glyph) at the tabs row's right end, above the hairline (LegalTabs' `status` slot, `.lgt-status-pill`): it is also the way out to the portal — with an act open it opens THAT act there, otherwise the portal itself. The act's own "On the portal" button is gone (the kind line keeps Save offline). Beside it, with an act open, a SECOND pill says whether the copy on this machine is what the portal has: an act read from the archive is checked in the background (`fetchActLive` + `sameAct` — text and in-force date; `actSync`), a copy that MATCHES is simply "Live from the portal" (the one success pill, with a tick — "From your copy on this machine" is never shown for a copy that is the portal's); one that differs keeps "From your copy on this machine" and gets "Differs from the portal - click to sync" beside it (filled danger, a button: `syncAct` puts the portal's version on screen and on disk); one just read live matches by definition; not yet checked or unreachable shows "From your copy on this machine" alone. The masthead's figures (Kept here / On this machine) are kept across visits in a module-level cache and drawn at a FIXED width (`.lg-mast-meta > div`, tabular numerals), because they arrive after the first paint and, growing, used to narrow the description beside them and shift the whole page down a line. **HISTORY — EVERY TAB HAS ONE** (`lib/tabHistory.js`: one log per tab in localStorage, `docvex:history:<tab>:v1` — `listHistory(tab)` / `logHistory(tab, { kind: 'search' | 'open', label, detail, data, dedupe })` / `clearHistory(tab)`, ≤400 entries; `lib/legislationHistory.js` is this tab's wrapper over it, keeping its older key `docvex:legislation:history:v1` and its own entry shape — `logSearch`: form + count + source, `logOpen`: the record without its text; Court files logs its searches and dockets and every file opened (by number + court, re-searched on pick), ANAF its lookups and, for a list of ≤5, each company found, CAEN every code opened, the Newsletter every briefing opened (picking one opens its act, else finds the briefing again), the placeholder tabs an empty log): every search run and every thing opened on this device, with the time; the shared **`components/HistoryMenu`** — `HistoryButton({ tab, onPick, renderEntry?, extra?, emptyText?, tip? })`, the tool button at the bar's FAR RIGHT, past the search (LegalTabs' `trailing` slot), which hangs a compact DROPDOWN under itself (`HistoryMenu`, portalled, placed from the button's rect with its right edge on the button's, the bar's dropdown ground, `.lg-hist`; a click elsewhere, Escape or a page scroll closes it — not a modal) laid out as the Advisor's thread — in the order they happened, oldest at the top and opened scrolled to the newest, so a search stands ABOVE what it opened; searches as the user's bubbles at the right with the time under, things opened as the answers at the left, day dividers — each a button (`onPick(entry)`: a search runs again, a thing opens again; an entry is drawn from its `label` / `detail` unless the tab passes `renderEntry`), with Clear (the log) and the tab's own `extra` head buttons (the Legislation tab's Kept acts — forget the archive's whole acts) in its head; the dropdown's CSS is `HistoryMenu.css`, classes still `lg-hist*`. The page lists RESULTS ONLY, once a search has run: the "Recently viewed" list is gone (History is where what was looked at lives). **With an act OPEN the tab bar's second line is the act's**: the words box FINDS INSIDE THE ACT (`find` / `findAt`: every occurrence marked where it stands, `.lg-hit`, the current one in the accent and scrolled to the middle; Enter or Tab = next, Shift+Enter / Shift+Tab = previous — `search.onKeyDown` in LegalTabs lets a page take a key first), a "Back" tool button stands before the bar (which stays, dice and all — submitting it closes the act and searches) and the match count with its arrows (`.lg-findnav`) after it, and the act has no find box of its own (the old `.lg-find` in the reader bar is gone; Save offline stands on the act's kind line ("LEGE nr. 133/2026"), at its right end, drawn as the tab bar's tool buttons (`.lgt-tool-btn`; `.lg-act-kindrow`; the reader bar above the act is gone) — a kept act shows nothing, the "Saved here" pill is gone — and the act stands on the page ground with no frame, corners or padding of its own). The act is never filtered to matching passages. The bar's own message (an empty form — "Give it a number, a year, or some words from the title." — a dead portal, a dice that drew nothing; `error`) stands on the tab bar's second line beside the bar (`.lg-barnote`), not in the strip under it. ONE LIST under the strip (`ActRow`): the last search's results (main's `archive-put` still stamps `openedAt` on a put `withText`, for the archive's own sake), both drawn as the same rows (kind/number/year, title, issuer, gazette, in-force date, "full text · N KB" / "details only", a forget button on kept acts), with a "Recently viewed" button to drop the results. **Diacritics:** a live search sends the words as typed, then — on an empty answer — with the cedilla spelling, then with none (`spellings` in `searchLegislation`; the portal's titles are written all three ways), and `fixDiacritics` (applied in `tidy` and on an act's text) puts back the comma-below Ș/Ț the portal loses as "?" ("Pre?edintele" → "Președintele"; before an "i" it is ț unless at a word start). |
| `Caen` (`/caen`) | **CAEN codes** — **A View dropdown** (List · Outline · Atlas, `docvex:caen:layout`) stands beside the Revision dropdown at the bar's far right: List is the search + tree + card; **Outline** (`CaenOutline` in `pages/CaenViews.jsx`, `cn-ol-`) is a sticky rail of the sections, the chosen section as a table of contents (divisions → groups → classes by type size; a group with one class of its own name is unlabelled) and the entry card sticky beside it; **Atlas** (`CaenAtlas`, `cn-at-`) is the sections as tiles with a weight bar (classes / the largest section's), then the section's divisions as masonry cards of their classes, the entry card floating sticky over the right side with Close. Both read the tree on show (Rev. 3 under All revs) and select through the page's `select` (URL + history), not the rail. the nomenclature, searchable and browsable (tree of sections → divisions → groups → classes, only the selected branch open), with each entry's parents, children, official notes (codes they point at are links), Rev. 2 origins, and a callout when the number meant something else in Rev. 2; a Rev. 2 code opens as what it WAS (“No longer in force”) with where it went. Copy puts “4100 – name” on the clipboard. **A revision switch** beside the view switch (`REVS`, `docvex:caen:rev`): Rev. 1 · Rev. 2 · Rev. 3 · All revs. Each revision is a whole tree (`trees` state, loaded once each through `loadCaenRev`; `data` = the active one), and the tree, the picker (keyed by revision, so it remounts) and the card read it alike; under All revs the tree and the picker walk Rev. 3 and the search answers from every loaded tree, newest first, each row tagged “Rev. N”. `?rev=1|2` on the URL names an old code's revision (`select(code, rev)`; left out, the tree on show). The card (`Detail({ trees, code, rev })`) reads the code's OWN revision's tree: an old revision opens under a “No longer in force” callout (its years and act, `REV_INFO`) with “Now (Rev. N+1)” / “Now split over” rows from `caenNext` and, when the same number is a different class in Rev. 3, a link to it; a class lists “Formerly, in Rev. N−1” from `caenPrev` (`PrevCodes`); a Rev. 3 number that meant something else in Rev. 2 keeps its callout (`resolveCaen`); rows of another revision carry a “Rev. N” tag and a press keeps that revision (`CodeList`'s `tagRev`). Only Rev. 3 has notes; the others say so. **Two views**, a List / Picker switch at the left of the Legislation tab bar's second line — under its hairline, before the search, pinned and frosted with it (`LegalTabs`' `tools` slot; `docvex:caen:view`): the list, and a **letter picker** in the manner of the PS3 Store's (`pages/CaenPicker.jsx` + `.css`, `cp-`; logic in `lib/caenPicker.js` — `pickerTree`, `stageOptions`, `ancestorsOf`, `makePickerReducer(tree)`, `keyToAction`, `LOAD_DELAY_MS` — the 380 ms pause the original spec asked for; kept in the lib, no longer shown, since the picker's status line was removed): the code is built in STAGES down the nomenclature's own structure — a column of the sections (A…V), then the chosen section's divisions, then groups, then classes — one row of columns standing on one top line, every box `--cp-boxtop` (170px) down so the selections sit in one row; ABOVE a box a few entries show, faded by distance, BELOW it every entry shows whole (each column's height is set inline to fit them — nothing under the selection is clipped or faded): the stages PASSED as faded columns (each level's entries, the entry chosen held in its box; a click on another goes back to it), then the column of this level's entries, each showing only what it ADDS to the code (`codeTail`: the letter, the division's two digits, then one digit per group / class), the boxed one as the cursor (always at the row's vertical middle), then, in the slots the NEXT stages will take, a faded PREVIEW column per level ahead (what the previous column's selection contains — the cursor's entry for the first, each preview's first entry for the next; a click on a preview entry goes down to it). A step back REMEMBERS the way (`ahead` in the model, `previewChain`): the previews keep centring on the entries the cursor was on, and going forward lands on them again. The stages passed and the entry under the cursor are named ONLY by the trail (the in-row stage codes and the name label over the row were tried and removed). (The model's `list` focus / `row` remain but this view never enters them); the levels AHEAD fade in over 220ms when their content changes (each preview is keyed by the cursor's entry, so it mounts afresh; a `from`-only keyframe, so it lands on its own faded opacity); the passed and live columns have no motion on a stage change, and nothing slides — the other motion is the entries gliding under the box as the selection moves (above the whole section, the CAEN page draws the highlighted entry's ancestors as PILLS — `Trail` in Caen.jsx, `cn-pill`, a fixed two-row height so the picker under it never moves — each taking the picker to that stage through the ref's `goTo(code)` (model action `goto`); the card's own breadcrumbs are off there, `crumbs={false}`). ↑/↓ move the cursor (the wheel too — over a passed column the first notch only goes back to that stage, over a preview it only steps into it — the next notches move there), → / Enter step INTO the entry (a class is picked instead; a stage holding a SINGLE entry is entered straight through, until a real choice or a lone class under the cursor), ←/⌫ step back a stage (cursor on the entry left), a passed stage's code is a button back to it (`jump`); Space picks a class; the keys work from anywhere on the page (one window listener; fields keep theirs); a typed letter jumps to a section and typed digits to the division / group / class they spell from any stage ("62" lands in division 62, "6210" puts the cursor on the class). Its key hints are English, like the page chrome (the picker itself shows only codes); its colours are the app's semantic tokens (the `--cp-*` names are this file's aliases — the dark-blue ground it began with was removed). The wheel moves the selection only with the pointer over a column (hovered: a hairline down each side, fading at top and bottom; every entry carries the app Tooltip with code and name; the card beside shows neither ancestors nor its own header — `crumbs={false}`, `head={false}` — since the columns and the pills do; it is the "N classes in it" list (`kids`, kept), the callouts, the notes and the source line); over the card it scrolls the page. The entry under the cursor — at EVERY level, section to class — has its card (the list view's `Detail`) INSIDE the picker, to the RIGHT of its columns (`children` → `.cp-side`; the columns area is a zero-height sticky anchor (`.cp-body`) with the columns drawn under it at a fixed height — min(70vh, 720px), no scroll, the wheel there selects — so the CARD alone decides the section's height and the page's scroll; the card is as tall as its content and never scrolls inside itself; under 760px the card goes below the columns), live (`onHighlight`); a pick (Space) sets the same `?code=` the list reads and scrolls the card into view. The key hints (`CaenPickerHints`, `cn-hints`) sit in a BOTTOM BAR (`.cn-bottombar`) that is the Files tab's `.fx-bottombar` to the letter — sticky a chrome-inset above the window's bottom edge, frosted, one `--rail-gap` off the rail — the page fills the scroller (`min-height: 100%`), the scroller drops its bottom padding under it and the seam strip rule is mirrored, all in Caen.css; keep the three (Files, Chat footer, this) in sync. Personal route beside Legislation, works on web too (the data is bundled). The Doc Viewer's paragraph dock lists every CAEN code a picked clause cites with its official name and the revision caveat (`ParaCaenCodes`); pressing a code opens it here in the MAIN window. `cn-` CSS in `Caen.css`. |
| `Research` (`/research`) | **A search engine of its OWN** over the law and the case — it reads the same portals as the Legislation tab and answers with the AI as the Advisor does, but NEVER NAVIGATES to either: everything it opens (the whole act, a court file, a company, a CAEN code, all results) opens in ITS DRAWER on the right (`pages/ResearchDrawer` on the shared `components/SideDrawer`: per device `docvex:research:drawer-w`, a view stack with Back, a press on the Research page keeps it open; views `act` (row's handed record, else searched by number/year; `loadAct` + `parseActText`, drawn in slices), `file`, `company`, `caen`, `results`). Formerly: the Legislation search and the Advisor as ONE search engine (neither tab is replaced), laid out AS THE ADVISOR (its classes: `.ai-chat-page`, the tab rail, the thread, the bottom-bar composer). The sidebar's FIRST row, outside every section (`.sidebar-cat--lead`), with a DROPDOWN of its chats like the Advisor's. CHATS ARE TABS: `lib/researchChats` = `createChatStore` from lib/advisorChats (now a factory; the Advisor's named exports are its instance), kept per USER (`docvex.research.v1.<user>.all`); the sidebar's two chat dropdowns share `useChatFold` + `renderChatEntry` in Sidebar.jsx (events `docvex:research-list-set` / `-listed` / `-focus` / `-activity`). Each question: every connected platform is asked (`platformsFor` / `searchPlatform`); a FIXED search (`pageForQuery` → an item) asks NO AI until asked — the panel opens with a Segmented choice (`RuleOptions`): the PORTAL's name (its answer, the default) or **AI summary** (made once on the first press: `recordText` in ResearchDrawer reads the WHOLE act / court file / company / CAEN code, the composer's model — Auto routes — writes it, `usageAction: 'research-summary'`, kept in the message as `summary` with the choice as `view`; the byline then says which model summarised it); with SEVERAL results the switch holds ONE CHOICE PER RESULT (≤6, `itemLabel`; the rest behind "all N results →" in the byline, which opens the drawer's list) then AI summary, which summarises the result picked last (`pick`, one summary per result in `summaries[idx]`; `fixedSummary` reads an older chat's single `summary` as the first result's); every choice is COLOUR-CODED to its platform (a dot, and the sliding pill in the picked one's tone — `--scope-tone` + `.lgb-scopes`, the Legislation results' recipe); the switch stands ON THE PANEL'S TOP EDGE (`.rs-fixed-switchwrap`, its vertical middle on the border, near-opaque frosted ground); a result is shown as its CARD, whose click opens it in the drawer — NO buttons: "Open the details" and "Read the whole act →" were both retired; an act's opening is a card too (`.rs-act-card`, the rows' `.lgb-row-main` look; a click that ends a text selection doesn't open it) and, cut off, fades out under a "Click to view more" pill (`.rs-act-more`, outside the masked article); the portal's answer stands in a rounded FROSTED PANEL of its own (`.rs-fixed`: bar frost + blur, hairline, card radius and shadow; its byline inside; a cut-off act fades out by a mask), drawn as the Legislation tab draws it (for an act: `takeRecord` → `loadAct` → `actHeading` + the first `parseActText` blocks under `.lg-act`, then Read the whole act, in the drawer); anything else goes to the AI — **the UNIFIED AI (lib/aiEngine, surface `research`)**: Auto, the portal records the question names, the project's files (switch) and legislation marked in the answer (components/AiAnswer; a click opens the drawer); it may NOT create or edit files. The byline names the model, Auto's reason, the portal records read and whether the project's files were in view — shown only while its reply is hovered (both surfaces; opacity, its room kept). (`exactMatchPage` moved to lib/aiEngine; an identifier alone still goes to the portal.) **Styled as the Advisor, piece for piece**: it wears the Advisor's FRAME — `.project-page-frame` around `.ai-chat-page` (AppShell adds that wrapper only to project routes, and the Advisor's gaps, full-bleed layout, sticky rail and bottom-bar composer are written against it: `.sv-single-scroll:has(.project-page-frame)` drops the scroller's padding, `.project-page-frame:has(.ai-chat-page)` the sheet's) — plus the search FINDING in the open chat (`useChatFind`, name `aichat`) and narrowing the rail (`highlightMatch`), the rail's pointer lights, a chat's right-click `.lgb-menu` and the keys (Ctrl+T / W / Shift+T / Tab / 1–9), day dividers, the thinking status (`components/AiThinking`, shared with the Advisor), and the composer's Project files switch as the Design system's `Toggle`, the model picker and the switch at the composer buttons' 25.6px. NO tabs in its mini header (a Chat · Debug pair was tried and removed at the user's request; each AI message still keeps its `timing`). **IT RUNS ON WHEN THE TAB IS LEFT** (`lib/researchRunner.js`, a module store like lib/scanRunner): the turn in each chat (`busy[threadId]` = its phase and model, numbered per chat — `beginTurn` / `isLive`, Stop = `stopTurn`, so a stopped turn writes nothing), the AI summaries being written and the reply to type out live there, not in the page's state; the turn's code keeps running after the page unmounts and writes into the chat store, so the answer is in the chat on the way back, and the page shows a turn still running. One turn at a time PER CHAT (another chat can run meanwhile). The APP SIDEBAR reads it: the Research row's `.nav-dot.is-spin` while anything runs, and a small spinner (`.nav-cat-spin`) at the start of every chat tab that is working (`renderChatEntry`'s `busy` may be a function of the chat id). The answers' Copy / Retry buttons were removed at the user's request. A new answer and a freshly written AI summary TYPE THEMSELVES OUT (`components/AiTypewriter` — the Advisor's typewriter moved into a shared component, which the Advisor now imports too; skipped under reduced motion; the chips, byline and actions wait for it). Cited acts still become chips (`findLawRefs` → `legislationQueryFor`) opening the act in the drawer; the byline says which model answered and whether the project's files were in view. **The composer's MODEL PICKER** (components/AiControls, shared with the Doc Viewer's advisor — ONE setting, `docvex:ai:model:v1`): Auto + every model; AUTO routes each question with the user's routing prompt (verbatim) on Haiku 4.5 (`usageAction: 'research-route'`) → JSON `{ model, reasoning }`. Under every answer a byline says which model answered (and Auto's reason), or that an exact match came from the portal with no AI. **Fable 5.1 is NOT in project-ai's `MODEL_ALLOW`** — picked or routed, it runs on Opus 5.5 and the byline says so; enabling it = add `claude-fable-5-1` there and redeploy (mind the undeployed `PLACEHOLDER_RULE` in the local index.ts). `rs-` CSS in `Research.css`. NOT verified in the app. |
| `Newsletter` (`/newsletter`) | Legal Newsfeed — Romanian legislation/compliance briefing. Typographically-led feed (masthead, AI-weekly line, Section/Impact/Search filters, day-grouped `ed-article` rows with category eyebrow + impact mark, AI-brief lead, "Affects …" meta line, per-item pin/save; a read entry is not faded and has no Mark read button — reading is recorded silently on open). Data is real (`legal_updates` + `legal_update_states` via `lib/legalFeed.js`); AI weekly line from `legal-ai`'s `digest` action, cached 1 h (`docvex:legal-digest:v1`), with a local fallback when the AI key isn't configured. Public personal route (not behind `ProtectedRoute`), reached from the **Personal** sidebar section. Styles `ed-`-prefixed in `Newsletter.css`. |

Other root pages (read source for depth):
- `Admin` (`/admin`) — "Developer Console": `app_services` inventory tracker, mailbox-intelligence panel, live platform metrics (`get_admin_stats`), destructive-ops danger zone. App-admin-gated (`app_admins` allowlist / `is_app_admin`).
- `Settings` (`/settings`) — app preferences (theme, display scale, thumbnails, file view, reduce motion, token-usage indicator, language) + the Electron "projects folder" picker.
- `Mail` (`/mail`) — personal inbox with real Gmail/Outlook OAuth sync (`mail-sync` / `mail-callback`); AI-drafts replies in the user's voice with tone/length controls.
- `Debug` (`/debug`, dev-only), `DocViewer` (`/doc-viewer`).

### Project-scoped (`src/pages/Projects/`)

| Page | Purpose |
| --- | --- |
| `ProjectList` | "Editorial Dossier" layout — documents-masthead header (accent eyebrow + big display "Projects." title), a **Recently opened** featured-card tier (last-7-days from `recentProjects.js`) and **All projects** (full list). Member avatar stacks from `get_member_profiles`, per-project accent derived from the id hash. All `pjx-`-prefixed CSS in `ProjectList.css`. |
| `ProjectCreate` | New-project form. |
| `ProjectOverview` | `/projects/:id` landing — `pjd-`-prefixed "Dossier" shell: back-link, hero (serif name + description), a 4-cell meta strip (Files / Members / Joined / Last activity), tab bar **Overview · Members · Roles · AI · Settings**. **Overview**: usage gauges + real Team list + placeholder activity timeline. **AI**: project-context textarea (`projects.ai_context`, admin-writable) + usage stats backed by `project_ai_usage` / `get_project_ai_usage` (real, but zero until AI features log requests). **Members / Settings**: real member management + invites, rename/description + danger zone. **Roles**: `RolesDossier` — role-catalog cards with per-role headcounts + a capability-matrix table wired to `lib/customRoles` (tri-state inherit/grant/revoke, optimistic overlay + debounced persist), `CustomRoleEditor` for create/delete. Chrome + Roles styles in `ProjectDossier.css`. |
| `ProjectDashboard` | Tabbed project dashboard (Members via `TeamTree`, Activity). Tab persistence via `useSearchParams ?tab=`. The former "Pending edits" / version-control tab (change-request review) was removed with the branching system (migration 031). |
| `ProjectFiles` | Local-files page — folder picker, listing, hashing, sidecar reconciliation (`lib/localBranchMeta.js`), search/nav, every action handler. Presentation via `components/FilesWorkspace`. |
| `ProjectChat` | Team + Private (DM) chat — split-pane layout. **Team**: message thread + collapsible right rail (**Pinned / Threads / Mentions / Files** sub-tabs). **Private**: 3-column DM pane (member list · thread · shared-files rail). Bubbled messages with @mention chips, day dividers, attachment cards, typing dots; per-message hover actions (react / reply-in-thread / pin, + edit/delete on own); reactions strip, thread pill, header search, inline edit, scroll-to-latest pill. Data: `chat_messages` (+ `parent_id`, `pinned_at`/`pinned_by`, `chat_message_reactions` from migration 026; pinning via `set_chat_message_pin`). Lib: `src/lib/chat.js` (`listThreadReplies`, `listProjectReplies`, `sendThreadReply`, `setChatMessagePin`, `listReactionsForProject`, `toggleReaction`, `subscribeReactions`) + `src/lib/privateMessages.js`. Styles in `ProjectChatVariantB.css` (`dvx-`/`vb-`-prefixed), full-height via `.project-page-frame:has(.dvx-chat.vb-chat)`. Renders **chromeless + flush** like Files (`/chat` is in both `CHROMELESS_FULLSCREEN_ROUTES` and `FLUSH_CONTENT_ROUTES`): a big `.dvx-mh` masthead + a sticky single-row `.dvx-toolbar` mini-header (tabs + chrome tools), the composer portalled into the SplitView footer (`usePaneChromeFooterEl`), and an internal `.dvx-scroll-area` block scroll container (the sticky toolbar must be a *direct* child of the scroller, not a flex item, or it won't pin). The toolbar gets `.is-pinned` (rect-based detection: bar.top − scroller.top ≤ 8) which paints the `--bg-sidebar` background only while actually stuck at the top. |
| `ProjectGenerate / ProjectAutomate / ProjectClients` | Stubs for future features. |
| `ProjectTodos` | To-do list stub. |
| `TeamTree` | Org-chart view of project members + roles. |
| `InviteAccept` | Token-driven invite acceptance; public so unauthenticated invitees can land here and bounce through `/auth`. |

**The Advisor answers with its SOURCES** (`lib/aiSources.js`, 2026-09-28 — the Insights question box's features, ADDED on top of the Advisor's rules, none replaced): `withSourcesRule` states as the opening exchange that an answer drawn from the project's files ends with a ```sources fenced block (JSON `[{ file, why }]`, ≤8), that it names people / amounts / dates / identifiers as written, says when files disagree or what is missing, and carries `AUTHORITY_RULE` (lib/docAuthority — favour the most official document); the digest's per-file understanding now opens with `Official: <rank> · <document type>` (aiProjectContext). ProjectAI strips the block from the text first (`splitSources`, before edits and choices), matches the names to the project listing (`resolveSources`) and draws them under the reply as **From** chips (`.aichat-sources`) that open the file in the Doc Viewer, with what each file says beside it. **The rules are FOLDED into one exchange** (`foldRuleExchanges`, both advisors): the sources, choices and edit rules used to go as three separate user-states-a-rule / assistant-agrees exchanges, which taught the model to answer a question with "Understood, I'll follow your rules…"; now they are one opening exchange said to be already agreed, and the last message ends with a note to answer it directly and never acknowledge the rules. An empty chat's prompts start with four questions about THIS case (`caseStarterPrompts`, from the people the scan read), ahead of the general ones. NOT verified in the app. **The AI EDITS project files** (`lib/aiFileEdits.js` + `components/AiEdits`, both advisors): the server tools can only write a NEW Office document or ask, so a change to an EXISTING file travels as a ```docvex-edit fenced JSON block at the end of the reply (`EDIT_RULE`, the conversation's opening exchange via `withEditRule`, says the AI CAN edit and how): a data collection (.dvc) takes `record` (fields merged, null removes), `facts` (upserted by label, source 'AI edit'), `summary` / `title`; a text file `replace` [{find, with}] or `content`; a .docx `replace` [{find: whole paragraph, with}] rewritten IN PLACE (lib/docxRewrite). `applyReplyEdits` applies them when the reply arrives (the file overwritten via writeFiles, `notifyFilesChanged`), the block is hidden from the text, and a card under the reply lists each file's changes (or why not) with **Undo** — the previous bytes kept in memory for the session (`keepResults` / `runUndo`; a saved thread keeps the report, not the undo). Data collections are therefore no longer strictly read-only; `DataCollectionView` re-reads on `docvex:files-changed`. NOT verified in the app; a later AI scan may re-derive what an AI edit changed. **An unanswered prompt is never resent** (`dropUnanswered` in lib/aiChoices, used by the Advisor's `toApiMessages` and every Doc Viewer advisor path — generate, ask, paragraph): a user message followed by another user message (its reply failed or was stopped) is dropped from what goes to the model — sent on as two user turns, or MERGED into one by `mergeStringTurns`, the model answered the old question instead of the new one. **The AI asks when a question is ambiguous** (the CLARIFY RULE, part of `CHOICES_RULE`): when the project holds several of what was asked about — a person's identity-document address vs. a rented home, similar names, several contracts / versions / dates / amounts — it asks which one in a sentence and offers each candidate as a choice, named with its source; it answers directly when only one fits or one was named. **Clickable answers in AI replies** — a FIXED RULE (`CHOICES_RULE`, stated as every conversation's opening exchange by `withChoicesRule` and repeated as a one-line reminder on the latest message) requires every question / offer to end with the ```choices block; detection is the safety net, and also reads options written as 2–6 short PARAGRAPHS under a short line ending in ':' that offers something (`OFFERS`: opțiuni, variante, poți, pot…, options, would you like…) — (`lib/aiChoices.js` + `components/AiChoices`): when a reply asks something or offers options, they become pills under the LATEST reply (once it has finished typing) and pressing one sends it as the answer — in the Doc Viewer advisor and the Advisor tab. `splitChoices(text)` takes them OUT of the text: an explicit ```choices fenced block at the end (which `CHOICES_STEER`, riding on every outgoing turn but the paragraph turn, asks the model to write), or a short list of 2–8 items at the end of a reply that asks something (a '?' just before or after it). A list with no question stays text. **The Advisor page SCROLLS AS ONE PAGE and wears the Design system's header** (ProjectAI.jsx + the "THE PAGE SCROLLS" block at the foot of ProjectAIChat.css, scoped `:not(.dv-advisor-chat)` because the Doc Viewer's advisor reuses `.ai-chat-page`): `PageMasthead` (scrolls away), then `LegalTabs standalone` as the mini header (`.aichat-bar`: `ownTabs` Chat / Debug, the conversations toggle in `tools`, the chat search as its search box — a find over the open chat, Ctrl/⌘+F — its frosted ground drawn only while stuck, as everywhere); `scrollRef` is a getter returning the page's scroller (`.sv-single-scroll`), the thread its own `threadRef`; the chat list is sticky under the bar to one inset above the window's foot (`--airail-top` / `--airail-h`, measured), the shell clips only sideways (`overflow-x: clip`, not a scroll container), the composer sticks one inset above the window's foot AND IS THE DESIGN SYSTEM'S BOTTOM BAR (the gallery's "Bottom bar" = .fx-bottombar / .cn-bottombar: frosted ground on a pseudo-layer behind the content so typing never re-runs the blur, 1.5px flat border, bar radius, `--mini-seam-shadow-down`; the input box inside drops its own frame; focus turns the bar's edge accent) — standing ONE --rail-gap from the APP SIDEBAR when the chat list is hidden (toggled or listed in the sidebar), level with the mini header, its padding giving the distance back; beside a shown list it starts at the thread column's edge. The shell no longer clips sideways (a hidden list is opacity 0 + visibility hidden) — that clip had shaved the bar's left edge, the overlay thread scrollbar is gone. An EMPTY chat (`.is-empty-chat`: nothing said yet) FITS THE WINDOW and does not scroll — the scroller locked, the shell / list / column the height left under the header (`--aifit-h`, measured) — and its greeting is the Design system's empty state (`.lss-card`: thin-stroke mark, title, muted line; LegalSourceStub.css imported). The composer's text starts ~8px in (bar 6.4px + text 1.6px) and the toolbar is drawn 4.8px left so the attach glyph lines up with it. Under the greeting, LAWYER PROMPTS (`lib/advisorPrompts.js`, ~140) as an UNEVEN GRID of the reply pills (`.aichat-starters`: wrapping, each as wide as its words, centred) scrolling in a box of its own with faded edges and ENDLESS — `nextPrompts(prev, n)` appends fresh shuffles as a sentinel nears (IntersectionObserver, capped ~600); a new order on every visit; a click sends it. It follows the LEGISLATION TAB'S CONTENT RULES (the Design system's page layout): the chat list's left edge pulled out to the header divider's left end (`--ds-divider-pull`, inline on the rail with its width = list + pull), its items padded back onto the content edge, `--ds-head-gap` under the bar, one `--ds-page-inset` above the foot, `--ds-list-gap` to the content (the resize band); the mini header's gap fade is back; the window's tall bottom fade gives way to the solid seam strip under the composer bar, as on the other bottom-bar pages (Files, CAEN), so it never bleaches the bar's frost. NOT verified in the app. **The Advisor's CHATS ARE TABS, exactly as the Legislation tab's** (`lib/advisorChats.js`, a store outside React like lib/legalBrowser, bound per user + project by the page AND the app sidebar — `bindChats`; the thread array stays at `docvex.aichat.v3.<user>.<project>` for account sync, its ORDER now the tabs' order, pinned first; the chat on show at `docvex.aichat.active.v1.…`): ONE blank chat at most (`isBlankChat`, never listed — the rail's **New chat** item, the Search tab's twin, stands for it; `openNewChat`), `closeChat` (× / middle-click / Ctrl+W; `markGone` for sync; kept in `closed` for **Reopen closed chat** / Ctrl+Shift+T this session), `closeOtherChats`, `toggleChatPin`, `moveChat` (drag; takes the pinned state of where it lands), `chatMeta` (the two lines: 'Advisor · <when>' / thinking…, the title). The APP SIDEBAR's Advisor entry is the Legislation entry's twin (`renderAdvisorEntry` in Sidebar.jsx, `fold: 'advisor'`, `docvex.sidebar.advisorOpen`): the chats listed Pinned / Open with the same rows, FadeText, × close, drag + FLIP, the same hover pill (`chatTabPill`: the last thing said); the row click on the Advisor opens a new chat (`docvex:advisor-focus` puts the caret in the composer); `docvex:advisor-list-set` / `docvex:advisor-listed` + `window.__docvexAdvisorListed` are the Legislation handshake — while the sidebar lists the chats the page's rail steps aside. The page's rail (`.aichat-rail.is-tabrail`) keeps its own box (width, resizer, hide toggle) but wears the Legislation rail's surface and contents (`.lgb-rail-head` with the count and the hand-to-sidebar switch, `.lgb-rail-list`, `.lg-rail-item.lgb-rtab` two-line tabs with the dot / spinner, dividers, `.lgb-rail-end`), the right-click `.lgb-menu` (New chat, Pin, Close, Close other chats, Reopen closed chat) and the keys (Ctrl+T / W / Shift+T / Tab / 1–9). NOT verified in the app. Newer project-scoped page (read source for depth): `ProjectAI` (`/ai`) — the project **AI chat**, backed by the `project-ai` Edge Function's `ask` action (+ `project_ai_usage` logging). Laid out exactly like the Chat tab (same `.dvx-mh` masthead / sticky `.dvx-toolbar` mini header / `.dvx-scroll-area` page scroller / footer-docked `.dvx-composer`): two tabs — **Chat** (a left rail of saved conversations with create/delete, per user+project in localStorage `docvex.aichat.v3.*`, plus the thread + composer) and **Debug** (intentionally empty). Labelled **Advisor** in the sidebar / pane nav / masthead. The assistant sees the whole project — and so does the DOC VIEWER'S advisor, on EVERY turn (`buildKnowledge` in DocViewer.jsx: the OPEN FILE's full contents — a data collection rendered readable by `collectionToText` / `readCollectionText`, any other file its saved reading, rendered text or extracted text, ≤40k chars — plus the digest, cached 60 s, riding on the outgoing copy of the last user message, the paragraph turn's frame included; a [Meta] line forbids answering 'I can't see that file' about anything present). The digest now also carries EVERY DATA COLLECTION in full (record, people, contacts, facts with sources, timeline, source files, connections, names, linked collections, face matches) and what the AI scan UNDERSTOOD of each file (the `understanding` facet), total cap 110k chars. `lib/aiProjectContext.js` builds a size-capped digest (project card + `ai_context`, members, the local-folder file inventory with cached `aiFileIndex` descriptions, the last team-chat messages — DMs excluded, the case timeline, OCR snippets, captions and file metadata) that rides transiently on each turn's last user message (never persisted into the thread, cached ~60 s). On top of that, attached (paperclip / drag-from-Files) or name-mentioned files get their full text extracted (`lib/extractFileText`) and inlined. The advisor can also CREATE files in the Files tab (Doc-Viewer-generate-style): turns run with `docTools: true`, `write_document` results save into the project folder (`localFolderApi.writeFiles` + `notifyFilesChanged`) in **any format**: docx/pptx/xlsx/pdf are BUILT from the model's prose (`buildDocumentBlobSmart`), a `.dvx` is parsed into a real identity record first (`parseIdentity` + `identityBlob`, so it opens in the record form rather than as loose JSON), and every other text format (txt/md/csv/json/xml/html/srt/code…) is written VERBATIM — the extension on the filename in the `[file: …]` header is what decides, not the tool's four-way `kind`. Binary media (png/mp3/zip…) is refused by name with a reply saying why. The steer (`FILE_STEER` + `dvxSteer`, built from `fieldsFor`) tells the model all of this, including the record's own keys. Every outcome lands a message in the thread with a Files-tab-style card for the file (`applyAiResult` wraps the work so a throw can't leave the advisor spinning with nothing said), and `ask_user` pauses into an inline `AskUserPanel` above the composer when the model is unsure whether/what to create (steer note in `FILE_STEER`). Styles in `ProjectAIChat.css` (+ the `.ai-hub` bubbles in `ProjectAI.css`); icons in `Projects/aiHub.jsx`. (The former AI hub with its five tools was removed; `ProjectAITools.jsx` remains on disk, orphaned. `components/ExtractionsPanel` and `lib/extractionHistory.js` still exist but are no longer mounted by any page.)

Shared layout: `ProjectScoped.css` provides the standard project page frame (sticky header, content container).

**Legislation speed, second pass (2026-09-27).** THE ACT IS DRAWN WITHOUT STOPPING THE APP: `ActBody` in pages/Legislation.jsx — every node of the portal's tree is a memoised `ActNode` (plain-text blocks `PlainBlock`), compared on its node, the find, whether the current match is inside it (`cur`) and the table switches, so a find step or a re-render redraws only what changed; the act is revealed in pre-order SLICES (70 nodes at once, then 180 per slice in a `startTransition`, so React yields between nodes), with a `LoadingNote` spinner at its foot until done; matches are numbered in `actTreeStrings`' order so the field's count and the marks agree; `refsOf` / `onRef` are stable (`useCallback` + a ref). `LoadingNote` (spinner + line, shown only after a short delay) also stands for Reading the act and for the portal's layout still coming (`pageLoading`); the layout is parsed on idle and handed over in a transition. **Nothing is re-fetched on entering a tab:** `listArchive` is memoised until a put / forget / clear (`archivePut`, `archiveChanged`) — every platform page lists it for the masthead's figures; the results page reads answers that have ARRIVED synchronously (`peekAnswer` in lib/legalSearch; `useSerp` re-reads on a tick) so it paints with no searching pass. **RELOAD:** every tab has one (beside ×, in the address row after Forward, the tab menu, F5 / Ctrl+R) — `reloadTab` in lib/legalBrowser: a results page drops its answers (`forgetAnswers`) and bumps its `refresh`; an item page calls the platform page's reloader, which `LegalWorkspace` registers from its `onReload` prop (`registerReloader`; a reload for a page not mounted yet waits for it) — Legislation re-asks the portal for the open act and its layout (`forgetLegislationSession`), else re-runs the search; Court files re-checks the open file (`checkFile`) else re-runs the search / docket; ANAF re-checks the company (`checkCompany`) else re-runs the lookup; CAEN has none (bundled). The tab shows its spinner meanwhile. **The app sidebar's Legislation entry, with tabs open, is drawn as the System section** (`.nav-fold.is-cat`): a FAINT WHITE GROUND wraps the row and its tabs (kept subtle — Ink: text colour 2% + a 4% ring; Cream: the card ground at 35% + the border at half strength; 3px padding; none when the rail is collapsed); its tabs are ordinary `.nav-item` rows (`.nav-cat-item`, NO dot — the platform named by its SOURCE ADDRESS — "anaf.ro · SRL", "legislatie.just.ro · LEGE" (`siteName` / `ownKind` from pageMeta, the address in lower case and in the platform's colour, a size up (0.6rem, 600) from the small capitals, the selected row included); the text uses the row's whole width, room being made for the × only while the row is hovered / focused) in a `.sidebar-cat-items` list, the Pinned / Open groups headed by `.sidebar-cat-label`; the selection is one row — the tab on show, else the Legislation row (`notActive` on the row). The `.is-wrapped` / `.has-sub-active` / `.has-row-active` rules are no longer used. **While that dropdown is LISTING the tabs** (expanded, with tabs, the sidebar not collapsed) the Legislation tab does NOT draw its own vertical rail of them: Sidebar announces it (`window.__docvexLegalListed` + `docvex:legal-listed`), LegalWorkspace's `useSidebarListsTabs` reads it with the collapsed state (`docvex:sidebar-state`); folding the dropdown or collapsing the sidebar brings the rail back. The two are ONE SWITCH both ways: the rail's head has a "Show these tabs in the app sidebar" button (`docvex:legal-list-set` → Sidebar opens the dropdown, which hides the rail). The tabs-across-the-top strip is unaffected. THE RAIL COMES AND GOES ANIMATED (`useRailPresence` in LegalWorkspace): it narrows to nothing while fading and the page glides over (`.lgb-rail.is-folded` / `.is-animating`, 300ms, then unmounted), and grows back the same way; no motion on the page's first frame or under reduced motion. THE SEARCH SCREEN (a new tab): its field is IN THE PAGE, under the line of text (`AddressRow bare` in NewTabPage — the field, dice and where-Enter-goes pill, no back / forward / reload), and there is NO mini header at all — THE MINI HEADER IS DRAWN ONLY WITH SOMETHING BOTH ABOVE AND UNDER ITS DIVIDER (the address row above; the scopes, History's Clear or an item's find under), which every page but the search screen has; with the tabs across the top the strip stands in the page as a plain row (`.lgb-strip-plain`) (`barShown`; the rail then measures `--lgb-bar-h` as 0 and sticks at the inset). The masthead's **i** (what is kept on this machine) stands directly above the divider under the masthead's text: at the address row's right end (`AddressRow extra`) wherever there is an address row — that row's hairline being the divider — and in the masthead on the search screen. On the search screen the rail stands the head gap (8px) under the masthead's divider, not the page's 20px. THE SEARCH SCREEN FITS THE WINDOW and does not scroll (`.lgb-root.is-search`): LegalWorkspace measures `--lgb-fit-h` (from the shell's top to one inset above the window's bottom, re-measured on resize), the page scroller is locked while it is on show (`.sv-single-scroll:has(.lgb-root.is-search)`), the shell takes exactly that height (its rail-sticking min-height and the column's foot air dropped; the rail, when other tabs are open, stretches to it), and a window too short for the content scrolls the column inside, never the page. In the app sidebar's list every line shows ALL its text, the label taking the row's whole width, and a line FADES OUT at the row's edge only when it is really too long for it — `FadeText` in Sidebar.jsx measures it; hovering a tab's × lights the row it would close with a faint WHITE tint (Ink: the text colour at 7% + a 16% ring; Cream: the card ground + the border) (`.nav-cat-row:has(.doc-tab-close:hover)`) (ResizeObserver → `.is-clipped` → the mask); no "…". FOLDING / UNFOLDING that list is ANIMATED: while there are tabs the list stays mounted inside `.nav-cat-fold` (inert when folded), its height running 0fr ↔ 1fr on a grid row with a fade, and the ground around the entry fading with it (`.nav-fold` transitions background, border and padding); none under reduced motion.

**Route loading (2026-09-27).** Every main-window page in AppRoutes is `page(factory, { warm })` — React.lazy with a memory: once its chunk has loaded it renders with no Suspense at all. `preloadRoutes()` (AppShell, once signed in) warms every page's chunk one at a time on idle, the Legislation platforms first (`warm: 1`); `warmLegalData()` (`lib/legalWarm.js`) warms CAEN (tree + notes) and the platform libraries. A page still loading suspends INSIDE the content area (AppShell's own Suspense around the Outlet) — the sidebar never blanks. CAEN keeps what has loaded readable synchronously (`peekCaenTrees` / `peekCaenRev` / `peekCaenNotes`), which the CAEN page, CaenModal and CaenCard start from, so they paint with their data on the first frame. A new lazy page should use `page(...)`.

## Component patterns

All components live in `src/components/` with a sibling `.css` file.

### Modals

`ConfirmModal` is the base shape (title / body / confirm + cancel). Domain modals (`DeleteProjectModal`, `DeleteAccountModal`, `InviteMemberModal`, `ChangeMemberRoleModal`, `RemoveMemberModal`, `ReportProblemModal`) follow the same z-index conventions and overlay scrim (`var(--overlay-scrim)`). Toasts render at `z 9999` so they pop over any modal.

### Role gating

`RoleGate` renders children only when the user's role meets `minRole` (integer rank: viewer 0 → owner 3).
`RoleLocked` is the alternative pattern requested in user feedback: keep the feature rendered for everyone and overlay a "[role] only" mask for users who lack the role, so the layout is consistent and discoverable rather than disappearing.
`RoleBadge` is a coloured pill of the role name. `CustomRoleEditor` drives create/edit of custom roles; `RolesDossier` is the current capability-matrix surface on ProjectOverview (the older grid-based `RoleCapabilityMatrix` stays on disk, unused there).

### Tooltip + morph-pill

`Tooltip` is a cursor-following pill — fixed-position, `transform: translate(x, y)` updated on `mousemove`, animated via `transition: transform 45ms ease-out` (re-targets per move, no queue). Trigger wrapper uses `display: contents` so it doesn't add a layout box. It opens on hover and on **`:focus-visible` only** — `onFocus` early-returns unless `target.matches(':focus-visible')`, so a mouse/programmatic focus doesn't pop the pill at a stale screen location. A window `pointermove` safety net (gated on a `shown` flag) hides it when the pointer leaves the trigger node (`node.contains(under)`), covering missed `mouseleave`s.

**Use `<Tooltip>`, not native `title=`.** Hover hints throughout the app go through this component (DocViewer, FilesWorkspace, AppShell, AskUserPanel, Sidebar, SplitView, TokenUsagePill, TitleBar). The only remaining `title=` usages are component *props* (PageMasthead/ConfirmModal/DangerRow/SettingCard/StreamDoc), not native HTML tooltips — don't reintroduce a raw `title=` attribute.

**Morph-pill FLIP recipe** (`useMorphPill.jsx`, used by FilesWorkspace tiles and elsewhere). Same DOM node serves as hover tooltip and right-click menu; the menu state adds an `.is-menu` modifier and a FLIP animation morphs between sizes. The CSS `transition: transform` is intentionally suppressed (`.is-menu { transition: none; }`) so a JS-set inline `transition: transform 220ms cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1)` can drive the morph without racing.

Recipe per right-click:
1. Snapshot the pre-menu rect (`oldPillRectRef = pillRef.current.getBoundingClientRect()`).
2. Toggle `menuMode = true`; React commits the larger menu layout.
3. `useLayoutEffect`: compute `sx = oldRect.width / newRect.width`, `sy = oldRect.height / newRect.height`. Snap `translate(x, y) scale(sx, sy)` with no transition. Force reflow.
4. Add `transition: transform 220ms`; set `translate(x, y) scale(1, 1)` — GPU-composable.

Dismissal: Escape, scroll (capture), outside `mousedown`, or mouseleave on the menu when `pointer-events: auto` is in effect.

### FilesWorkspace

`FilesWorkspace.jsx` + `.css` — presentation layer for the local Files page: file-explorer-style chrome, tile/list canvas (Ctrl+scroll zoom), `useMorphPill` hover/right-click menus per tile/row. Driven entirely by props from `ProjectFiles.jsx`; holds only local UI state (view mode/zoom, search, selection, menu/properties open). **Rubber-band selection** (Windows Explorer / macOS Finder; in the File explorer view only — never in Graph or Insights, `graphOn`): a press ANYWHERE on the screen (heard document-wide while Files is mounted; not on a file, button, field, menu, the app sidebar or the title bar) and a drag draws `.fx-marquee` (an accent wash in an accent edge, FIXED and portalled to the body; its corners kept in canvas coordinates so it follows the files as the page scrolls; the app SIDEBAR stays on top of it — the sidebar's rounded rect is cut out of it with an even-odd `clip-path: path()`) and selects every `[data-fx-id]` tile / row it touches as it grows — plain replaces the selection, Shift adds, Ctrl/Cmd toggles against it; the page scroller auto-scrolls near its top / bottom edge; a press that never moves 4px is an ordinary click, and the click that ENDS a drag is swallowed (capture, once) so the window-wide click-away doesn't clear what was just selected (`onCanvasMouseDown`). **It costs no frames**: no React state is set during the drag — the rectangle is one element made for it and moved by transform (`contain: strict`), every item's box is measured ONCE at the start in canvas coordinates (they move only with the scroll, read once a frame before anything is written), `is-selected` is toggled on the items themselves only where it changes, and the selection reaches `setMultiSel` on release. (Setting state per move re-rendered the whole page and every tile, then forced a layout to measure them all.) **Sort** (the header, beside Group by category): a solo `BarPicker` over `FX_SORTS` — Name A–Z / Z–A, Newest, **Recently opened** (the files last opened on this device first — `lib/recentFiles.js`, path → time in localStorage `docvex:files:opened:v1`, marked by FilesWorkspace's `openItem`, which every way of opening goes through; the list re-orders as a file is opened; never-opened ones follow, newest first), Oldest, Largest / Smallest, Type — kept with the view prefs (`sortBy`); folders and files are each ordered among themselves (folders first), ties fall back to the name, waiting phone files always lead, and by name archives stand next to the folders. It reads `sortTime` / `sortSize` on each item (ProjectFiles: a file's mtime and size, a folder's newest file and total size, a Trash item's deletion time, a phone file's arrival). **Portrait photos** get a taller tile: `usePhotoAspect` reads the loaded thumbnail's shape (a capture-phase `load` listener on the thumb box) and sets `--fx-thumb-ar` = height/width clamped between the usual 0.58 (the minimum — landscape tiles unchanged) and 4/3; remembered per item id (`thumbAspects`) so virtualised tiles don't jump; a tile is NOT stretched to its row's height — it wraps its own thumb + name (so does its hover / selection fill) and is centred in the row (`.fx-tile { align-self: center }`). All `fx-`-prefixed CSS. Currently being reworked alongside the migration-031 local-files pivot — read directly for the current tab/action structure.

### Status + theme

- **StatusBadge / StatusPicker** — user status enum (`online / away / dnd / offline`).
- **ThemePicker** — mock cards painted via `[data-theme="…"]` on each card so they preview in their own theme regardless of the app's active theme.

### Layout

- **AppShell** — wraps Sidebar + content; mounts the global ReportProblem modal, the `CursorSpotlight`, the `UpdateProgressBar`, and the `SplitView` content shell.
- **Flush-left layout + route sets.** `.app-chrome` uses `gap: 0` so page content sits **flush against the sidebar** (no gap); the window-edge inset (top/right/bottom) still comes from `.app-shell` padding, and right-side padding is kept so content doesn't hug the scrollbar. Two route sets drive per-route chrome:
  - `CHROMELESS_FULLSCREEN_ROUTES` (`SplitView.jsx`) — render WITHOUT the in-content chrome bar (page carries its own masthead): `/`, `/newsletter`, `/versions`, `/settings`, `/debug`, `/mail`, `/projects`, `/account`, `/files`, `/chat` (+ the exact `/projects/:id` overview).
  - `FLUSH_CONTENT_ROUTES` (`AppShell.jsx`) — full-bleed editorial surface (no rounded frame, negative margins to the window edges, flush to the rail): `/`, `/newsletter`, `/versions`, `/files`, `/chat` (+ the exact `/projects/:id` overview). Non-flush "card" pages get a flattened left edge (`border-left:none`, no left radius) so they still sit flush against the rail.
- **Gap to the sidebar.** No floating bar touches the sidebar rail: every mini header (fixed or pinned) and every footer (Chat composer, Files action bar) starts `--rail-gap` (= `--chrome-inset`, the gap the rail keeps from the window edge; AppShell.css) from the rail, and trims its own left padding by the same amount so its text stays on the content edge. Bars that used to be pulled flush (Activity toolbar, Newsletter / Versions headers, Chat toolbar + footer, Files pathbar + bottom bar) and the ones that floated a whole `--content-left-gap` off (`pmh-compact`, Mail, Admin, Dossier) all read it — change the gap there.
- **Unified mini-headers.** Every tab's compact sticky header is the same shape: a rounded `--bg-sidebar` section (matches the window top bar), `height: 40px`, `border-radius: 9.6px`, `top` offset by `--chrome-inset` (so it doesn't tuck behind the sidebar), right gap = the tab's content width, with `padding` matching Files. Per-surface class names: `pmh-compact` (PageMasthead — Activity/Versions/Newsletter/Settings/Debug) — drawn ONLY when the page passes `compactRight` (a status or an action): a bar holding just the title and the eyebrow beside it was removed, so Settings, Debug, Playbook and Roadmap no longer get one, `pjd-compact` (ProjectOverview), `dc-compact` (Admin), `fx-pathbar` (Files), `dvx-toolbar` (Chat). Each gets `.is-pinned`/`is-pinned` only when actually stuck at the top (rect-based detection), painting the bg then and staying transparent while scrolling under the masthead.
- **Sidebar** — 60 px collapsed, 220 px expanded on `:hover` / `.locked`. `.label` elements fade via opacity. Anything interactive that should respond when expanded needs `pointer-events: auto` in the `:hover` / `.locked` rule (see `.lock-btn`). Auth-aware footer swaps between Account NavLink + avatar / username / tier and a "Sign in" CTA. Has a cursor-following spotlight glow (see the zoom/native-event Conventions notes).
- **TitleBar** — custom frameless title bar (Electron), shared by the main window and every Doc Viewer window: brand (`DOCVEX | HUB` on the Hub, `DOCVEX | <project>` + member avatars in a project, `DOCVEX | <file name.ext>` in the Doc Viewer), update pill, actions (Privacy & security — opens `SecurityInfoModal`, formerly a row at the foot of the sidebar — then Report a problem, light/dark toggle; the Doc Viewer also gets its account as an avatar, since it has no sidebar — same hover card + menu as the Sidebar's account row via `components/AccountMenu` (`useAccountMenu`); from a viewer window, Account settings and Log out go to the MAIN window over `window:navigate-main` (`'/account'` / `'@logout'`, handled by `TrayNavigation` in App.jsx) and Log out then closes the viewer). The main window's sidebar narrow/widen toggle is the SAME burger as the Doc Viewer's side-panel toggle, at the far left of the bar: AppShell owns the state and listens for `docvex:sidebar-toggle`, announcing it back as `docvex:sidebar-state` (+ `window.__docvexSidebarCollapsed`) and window controls, driven by the `window:*` IPC. The Sidebar's account row (main window) is one hover area — avatar, name, email and the selected project's AI usage bar (`components/AiUsageMeter`: `useAiUsage` + `AiUsageBar`, no words). Hover shows a `useMorphPill` card with the account and the AI usage numbers (`components/AccountMenu`, shared with the Doc Viewer); a click morphs it into a menu — Account settings (`/account`) and Log out (confirmed inside the pill). There is no separate sign-out button any more. Layout reserves `--titlebar-h`. (`ProjectPickerPanel` was removed.)
- **LegalWorkspace** (`components/LegalWorkspace.jsx` + `.css`) — **DocVex · Source** (the address row's FIRST control, `ViewModeToggle` in LegalBrowser, `lib/legalViewMode.js`, per device, SOURCE BY DEFAULT at the user's request): an open item is shown AS ITS SERVICE GAVE IT (`SourceView` in LegalWorkspace: which service answered, the record as JSON in the service's own fields, an act's text as sent; Copy) or restyled by DocVex; each platform page passes its open item's raw data as `source` (Legislation: the Search record — `normalizeRecord` keeps it as a NON-enumerable `raw`, never archived; Court files: the Dosar record; ANAF: `normalizeCompany`'s `raw`; CAEN: the bundled entry + INS notes), and in Source the page's own view stays mounted but hidden so switching back keeps its state. Search results are not switched. **THE RAIL TOGGLE** (`RailToggle` in components/LegalTabs — the mini header's tool button `.lgt-tool-btn`, square, a panel glyph, PRESSED — accent on a soft accent ground and ring — while the list is shown) stands first on the bar's second line of the Legislation tab (its tabs; `docvex:legislation:rail-hidden`, `canRail` / `showRail` in LegalWorkspace) and of the Advisor (its chats): pressing it hides the list, or shows it — taking the list back from the app sidebar when the sidebar was listing it (`docvex:legal-list-set` / `docvex:advisor-list-set` with `open: false`), so the two stay one switch. **The dice stands APART from the search field, to its RIGHT** (its own rounded accent button, 6px off; `.lgb-omni` order / gap — LegalBrowser.css foot), and the SEARCH SCREEN (`NewTabPage`) wears the Design system: its head the gallery's EMPTY STATE (accent thin-stroke mark, 21px / 600 title, muted line) and the platforms the gallery's LIST ROWS (page ground, hairlines, no card; site + dot on the left, what it holds and its tries on the right). **THE LEGISLATION TAB IS A BROWSER (2026-09-27, from the "Legislation Unified Search" design):** the frame every platform page (/legislation, /portal-just, /anaf, /caen, the placeholders) renders is now browser chrome — `lib/legalBrowser.js` (the state: `{ tabs, active, closed, hist, layout }`, persisted at `docvex:legal-browser:v1`; a tab is a back/forward STACK of pages — `new`, `history`, `serp` `{ q, scope }`, or `item` `{ route, url, itemId, kind, title, addr, pending, baseline }`), `lib/legalSearch.js` (one query → a page: `pageForQuery` opens anything exact — an act by kind/number/year, a court file number, one CUI, a CAEN code — straight away, and sends everything else to a RESULTS page; `searchPlatform(platform, q)` asks one platform, cached per query for the session; a result row hands its record over by `?rid=` — `handRecord` / `takeRecord` — which Legislation.jsx and PortalJust.jsx open as they are, no search re-run) and `components/LegalBrowser.jsx` + `.css` (`lgb-`: `TabRail` / `TabStrip` (Pinned / Tabs, drag to reorder, right-click `TabMenu`: new, duplicate, pin, close, close others, reopen closed, tabs on the side / across the top), `AddressRow` (back / forward, the dice joined to the ONE field, a pill saying where Enter goes, the page's status pill; Enter here, Ctrl+Enter a new tab, Alt+Enter one behind), `NewTabPage`, `SerpPage` + `SerpScopes` (every connected platform asked at once, three per group under All, a scope per platform with counts), `HistoryPage`, `useBrowserKeys`: Ctrl+T / W / Shift+T / L / Tab / 1–9, Alt+← →; links follow browser rules — Ctrl/middle-click a new tab behind, Shift+click in front). LegalWorkspace lays out the masthead ("Legislation" + `KeptFigures` of the archive), the sticky bar (strip · address row · the page's line: its `tools` / `trailing` at the left — the scopes on a results page, Clear history on History — and the find at the right: the page's own find when it has one, else one over the tab's content), the rail and the main column (`NewTabPage` / `SerpPage` / `HistoryPage`, or the platform page's children for an item page). The platform pages are unchanged in shape: their `items` / `activeId` are published (`lib/workspaceItems`) and REPORTED to the tabs (`reportItems`): a tab bound to an item selects it when shown; an item the page opens itself (a citation, a row of its own list) becomes the tab's next page; a link arriving from elsewhere (`?nr=`, `?cui=`, `?code=`… — `arrive` / `arrivalLabel`) becomes a page of the active tab, pending until the page shows something new (≤6 s, `settlePending`). The arrival effect runs BEFORE the publish so its baseline is the item shown until then. `WorkspaceSearch` now draws only a page's foot once it has an answer (`asked=false` → nothing: the new-tab page is where searching starts); a page's `masthead` / `history` / `onSearch` props are ignored. The APP SIDEBAR's Legislation dropdown lists the browser TABS (Pinned / Open, `pageMeta` two lines, × closes; a lone blank tab lists nothing) — while one of them is the tab on show the fold is `.has-sub-active`: THAT tab takes the sidebar's selected-tab look (Ink accent tint + ring; Cream the lifted cognac pill) and the wrapper around the row and its list steps back to a faded white ground. On Legislation the ROW itself opens a SEARCH TAB (the blank one if there is one, else a new one, caret in the search — `onRowClick`); the fold chevron collapses the list; the row has its hover wash back inside the dropdown, INSET 3px from the wrapper's edges (its padding gives the 3px back so the icon and label don't move), and it is never faded — only the ground around it steps back; while LEGISLATION ITSELF is selected (the search tab on show, `.has-row-active`) the selection is the ROW's alone — its icon and label take the selected-tab look, inset 3px — and the wrapper takes the same faded ground — Debug's "Simulate items" no longer shows there. **The mini header IS the Design system's** (`LegalTabs`, the gallery's "Mini header", `.lgt-bar` — not a look-alike): LegalWorkspace renders `<LegalTabs standalone rows={[strip, AddressRow]} tools search />` straight after the masthead, so it pins and frosts itself (rect-based), draws the gap-strip fade (`MiniHeaderFade`) and the seam, and its row hairline takes over as the masthead's divider (LegalTabs.css drops the masthead's own). `rows` is LegalTabs' new slot: nodes drawn as its tab rows (`.lgt-row.lgt-row-own`). The page's controls and the find are its second line (`.lgt-line2`); a page with neither (a new tab) gets NO second line — LegalTabs' `line2={false}` — so the mini header is its rows alone. The address row's Ctrl + L hint is the search box's own key hint (`.lgt-search-kbd`: plain muted letters, ⌘ on a Mac). The frame only measures the bar (`--lgb-bar-h`, `--lgb-travel`) for the rail. The masthead no longer shows "Kept here / On this machine": they are in an **i** card at its right (`InfoButton` in LegalOmnibox — the platforms button's own `.lgo-info` + `.lgo-pop`, for any content; the popover is now the Design system's dropdown recipe: accent edge, the page ground lifted a touch, no shadow), with a line saying why acts are kept. A results page's scopes (All · Legislation · Court files · Companies · CAEN codes, with counts) are the Design system's **Segmented choice** (`components/RuleOptions`, its pill sliding to the choice; the labels alone, each platform named by its SOURCE ADDRESS (All · legislatie.just.ro · portal.just.ro · anaf.ro · insse.ro) — what it holds and its count are only in its tooltip; its pill is COLOUR-CODED — the chosen platform's own tone (`PLATFORMS[…].tone`, the colour of its dot in the tabs and the results: Legislation `--cat-update` violet, Court files `--info`, Companies `--success`, CAEN codes `--warning`), All the accent, the colour gliding with the pill. To its right a SECOND Segmented choice whose choices are what the PLATFORM PICKED is about (`FACETS` / `facetsFor(scope)` in lib/legalSearch, each option `{ id, label, tone, tip, test(row) }`, colour-coded): under All the branch of law (Penal · Civil · Other); legislatie.just.ro the kind of act (Laws · Emergency ordinances · Ordinances · Decisions · Orders · Other — `attrs.act` from `tipAct`); portal.just.ro the subject matter (Penal · Civil · Administrative · Labour · Business · Other — `attrs.cat` from the portal's category); anaf.ro the fiscal standing (VAT payers · Not VAT payers · Inactive); insse.ro the CAEN level (Sections · Divisions · Groups · Classes — `attrs.level`). UNDER ALL THERE IS NO SECOND SWITCH (removed at the user's request — `branchOf` answers 'all' there, so All never narrows); it appears once a platform is picked. Filtering is `inBranch(answer, facet, scope)`; THE RESULTS' PILLS WEAR THE FACETS' COLOURS (`facetTone(scope, id)`): an act carries its kind (Law · Emergency ordinance · Ordinance · Decision · Order) in its Legislation facet's tone and, when it has one, its branch (Penal · Civil) in the All facet's; a court file its category in the portal.just.ro facet's tone; a company VAT payer / Not a VAT payer / Inactive in the anaf.ro facets'; a CAEN code its level (Section · Division · Group · Class). A platform choice that answered NOTHING, and a facet with no result among what the platform choice answered, is FADED (`empty` on a RuleOptions option → `.pbk-rule-opt.is-empty`, NOT pickable — `aria-disabled`, no hover; judged only once everything in the choice has answered — nothing fades while searching), and each facet's tooltip gives its count. the platform switch's own tooltips count before it. Originally just All · Penal · Civil · Other ( kept PER PLATFORM CHOICE — `page.branches[scope]`, read through `branchOf(page)`, so switching platform brings back the branch picked there, and one never set is All): every result row carries `branch` (`branchOfText` — a court file by the portal's category, else its object; an act by its kind and title: criminal code / "infracțiuni" / criminal procedure vs the civil code / civil procedure; companies and CAEN codes are Other) and `useSerp(q, scope, branch)` hands back each answer narrowed (`inBranch`), so rows, counts and tooltips agree. The results' SUMMARY stands IN LINE WITH THE ADDRESS FIELD (`SerpSummary`, `.lgb-addr-found`; hidden while a new search is being typed) and says only what neither the field nor the heads do — under All the total ("1,023 results in all"), and "read as …" only when the words were read as something else; the query itself is in the field. The FIELD SIZES ITSELF to what it holds (the text, or the placeholder while empty — a hidden copy, `.lgb-field-measure`, measured plus the field's own chrome), never under 240px (`FIELD_MIN`), gliding to a new width and shrinking when the row runs out of room; the one standalone line left is "Nothing matches … on any connected platform" (under All every empty group is hidden)). **OPENINGS SETTLE BY ID**: every page opening by URL carries its own `pid`; `settlePending(route, pid)` finds THAT page and binds it to what the platform shows only if it is still on screen (the active tab's current page) — it used to bind whatever tab was active by then, which gave a tab another tab's item and broke switching; a page left behind is unbound again (`settled`), so selecting it opens it afresh (4s). The switch spinner ends only once the page is opening, shows the tab's item, or has settled with nothing. **The app sidebar's list REARRANGES by dragging** (HTML drag and drop on `.nav-cat-row`, `moveTab`): dropped on a tab it goes before it, taking that place's pinned state; the target is read off the pointer's height over the WHOLE list (`dropAt`: before the first tab whose middle is below it, else the end — per-row handlers left the gaps and headings to the list, which took each for "the end"); an accent line marks where; a drop that would not move anything is ignored; on release every row GLIDES from its old place to its new one (FLIP in a layout effect keyed on the tabs' order, 260ms). **CLICKING A TAB IS INSTANT** (`applyPage` in lib/legalBrowser): the selection is the store's and moves at once (rail, strip, app sidebar on that very frame); putting the tab's page on screen — the route change, the platform page showing its item — is scheduled after that frame and run in a `startTransition` (only the latest request runs), so React renders it in the background, the old page staying up. `switching` is set meanwhile: LegalWorkspace fades the content (`.lgb-main.is-switching`) and shows a sticky spinner (`SwitchSpinner`, after 140ms) until the page shows the tab (`endSwitch` from an effect; an 8s cap).
